On each tick whose main animation has the head-look bit (73 of Tor's entries, e.g. the run 3; not the idle 0 or the swings), the hero picks the target (the talk partner while a dialogue is open or the locked target, at their aim point; else the nearest live character in front, dot above 0.5, closer than 12.0 for Tor or 25.0 for Roberta, raised 1.5; else 5.0 ahead of the head), steps the controller from head joint 6, and the pose turns joint 6 and every joint below it (across the main, sub-part and face families) about the head's position so the head's forward axis points along the look direction with its up levelled to world up, the elevation mirrored back as the head step's negated pitch does. Without the bit the head keeps its animated pose. Not built: the animation-chosen side and reset targets of the target choice (Medium), the cell list B objects as targets (characters only), the spec's exact two-step head math (Medium-Low; this build's single rotation stands in for it), Roberta's hair chains after the look. Seen: with a target 5 to the side and 2 up (`NEOPETS_TRACE_LOOK_SIDE`), Tor runs with his head turned a quarter turn to that side and tilted up; straight ahead the head is level while running; the traced head forward axis matches the look direction (dot 1.000). Trace hooks: `NEOPETS_TRACE_LOOK`, `NEOPETS_TRACE_LOOK_SIDE`, `NEOPETS_NO_LOOK`. Tests: the rate cap, the limits, 32 calls from limit to limit, the pitch quirk and the reset.
- **Plague Serpent neck look (SPEC-0040 Behavior 23A mode 1; SPEC-0016 6A, Medium-High).** The neck joint list is read from the boot program (six s32 at 0x320F48: joints 6, 7, 8, 11, 14 and 17, as the spec says). `neopets_sim::look::neck_step`: A = acos(h_t . h_b) from joint 6 toward the hero and toward joint 17 (no clamp), the side test s, tau = -A/6 or +A/6, and each of the six angles snapping to tau within 0.01 or else stepping 0.01 only in the allowed direction (the spec's quirk: from 0 the neck moves only by the snap, and freezes otherwise). In state 0 (idle) the serpent steps its angles at the active hero from its animated pose; on any other tick they go back to 0. The pose turns each listed joint and the joints below it about the world up axis through the joint, in list order, on top of the animation. Trace hook `NEOPETS_TRACE_NECK`. Tests: the snap when the hero is nearly along the neck, the freeze when far to the side. Unverified: in a trace of pack 735 (the fight started with level state 8) the serpent never reached its idle state without the hero fighting, so the neck was not seen; whether it visibly turns in play is open in the spec (SPEC-0016).
- **Instanced-mesh draw distance (SPEC-0046 Per-frame draw order, Instanced meshes step 2; SPEC-0004 Instanced meshes).** A type-20 record's draw distance D1 (+0x1C) and fade length F (+0x18) are now read; each of its instances' mesh parts carries its instance translation and D1, and the part is hidden while the eye (the camera) is D1 or more from that translation (squared distances compared), alongside the zone gate. On disc D1 is 4000 in 52 records and 100 to 1000 in 15 (small props). Not built: the fade (F is 0 in all 67 records on disc) and the far mesh at D2 (no record has one). Seen: 909 and 1217 draw their nearby trees and props as before.
- **Instanced-mesh draw distance follows the Distant foliage setting (this build's display option).** The per-instance D1 cull now applies only when Display > Distant foliage is Original (the PS2's distance behaviour); with Full, this build's default, every instance draws at any distance, as before. The setting's help text now says Original also hides props past their draw distance.
- **Developer-menu boss fights (this build's tool; SPEC-0035 start-point confirm, SPEC-0049 and SPEC-0024 tables 11 and 12, SPEC-0058 walkthrough, SPEC-0062 atlas).** `DesignTables::stage_flags` and `stage_items` read table 11 (the progress flags of each stage) and table 12 (each stage's item grants) from resource 533. The developer menu (F1, HERO) has "Boss fight": Left and Right pick one of 13 fights (Juppie Act 1, the raider leader, Gnarfas, the Plague Serpent, the Black Knight, Juppie Act 3, the Ixi Chieftain Act 3, the Werelupe King, the Gelert Assassin, the Undead Draik, the Faerie Sisters, the Darkest Faerie, the cut Cloud Beast), and Enter rebuilds the game in that boss's world, as a new game whose flags are the stage rows of the start-point index the game's own chooser would use for that part of the story (index 0, 2, 5, 7 or 8) plus the walkthrough flags between that checkpoint and the fight (the SPEC-0058 step is cited per entry), so the world's Init takes the fight's branch; then the stage item grants (item 135 and 136 to 139 as Neopoints), the enlistment gear of step 7 for the Act 1 fights (sword 99, armour 120) when not already held, the equipment, full health and magic, and the hero placed at the fight's start trigger (or the world's arrival). The Cloud Beast entry also turns on its test spawn and places the hero in the arena (and sets the Act 3 Juppie fight as won, so Juppie is not loaded). `NEOPETS_TRACE_BOSS=n` starts a play trace as fight n. **Pack 985's AI-player activate callback (SPEC-0041 985 Init):** the streaming filter of its embedded block now refuses records by flag 281 (clear: only Illusen, Gnarfas and the Werelupe King; set: only the five Gelert Assassins and record 6), so the Gnarfas and Gelert fights no longer load each other's bosses (the walk-mesh assignments it also makes are not reproduced). Seen in traces (`NEOPETS_TRACE_BOSS` 0 to 12, 900 to 1200 ticks, the hero standing): Juppie Act 1's intro scene, the Plague Serpent's intro and attacks, Gnarfas, the Black Knight, the Ixi Chieftain, the Werelupe King, the Gelert Assassin, the Undead Draik, the Faerie Sisters and the Darkest Faerie controllers all running; the raider leader's fight is staged behind its henchmen (no boss activity in the first 1,200 ticks). Unverified: the menu entry was not clicked in the window; the flags are the minimum the walkthrough names, so side content and some world layouts may differ from a played-through save.
- **Spec update, 2026-10-28 (32 specs revised; this entry and the next ones follow it).** **Pack 1324's solved scene (SPEC-0041 1324, R-0103; reverses the last update):** the stage-1 plate callback, with all three stage-1 plates pressed and both stage-2 marks set, now runs the solved-scene helper after its camera return: the paired sounds 4 and 5, 2.0 s, flag 239, a 2.0 s fade out, sounds 4 and 5 stopped, FMV 23, a 0.5 s fade in, then the paired sounds and a 15-tick sleep ten times. Flag 239 is the scene-done word here (Init and Update read the flag), so 1344's trigger 1 (step 25) can now set 240. **Reeds (SPEC-0060 Cut, Reed state):** the cut launch direction is the normalized motion of the weapon point 2.5 along the blade's frame axis between this tick and the last (the blade axis when it has not moved); in the last fall state the piece's up axis turns to normalize(0.965 v + 0.035 up) every tick while |v.y| is above 0.001 (it stays in state 1 and is drawn), and is gone at once when |v.y| is 0.001 or less. Tests: the solved scene (flag 239 before FMV 23, ten closing chimes; none with one mark), the reed piece's end by launch (the spec's tick-11 vector passes 0.5 on tick 10 under plain f32 adds).
- **Spec update, target points (SPEC-0040 Behavior 20 item 6 and Open question 210, R-0102).** The Cloud Beast's target point is now its controller's override, the companion's pose point 1 (the stand-in's orb), and Roberta's homing shots and the hero's head look aim at the shared target point (render attachment point 7, the spec's default; no point from body height) instead of 1.5 above the target's feet. The candidate range was already strict 3D under 20.0. Checked against the update and found already matching: the swing trail's corrected ageing gate (code 8 ages, code 9 frozen: our trails age on play ticks, which stop while paused), the block footprint step (never built; pushes test only the block-to-block clearance), the 1148 desk palette (materials pick the palette at their own CBP, 848 here), Roberta's chain rest angles in radians, the Gelert's clone slots (ordinal 0 the boss, 1 to 4 clones) and its landing search over every navmesh with 10 tries. Not built yet: the kind-0x13 dialogue give's item-received notice (its visible form is unknown, SPEC-0022), the blade recoil against solid volumes and type-10 faces (SPEC-0028; its contact inputs are Medium and, read as the reference position, cannot reach a sphere: R-0118 filed).
- **Spec update, counter-attack code 5 (SPEC-0038 Behavior 14, R-0117).** Code 5 now holds when the enemy's sequencer is in state 4 (outer 2) and L1 is held in pad slot 0 (the held-button word's mask 0x0004, this tick's input; not a hero flag), so the 32 used slots with code 5 can counter while the player holds the guard button. Also checked against the update and already matching: the ground probe's 0.0 for a missing or empty cell and its miss value only for a non-empty cell with nothing sampled (SPEC-0018). Test: the existing counter-attack tests.
- **Spec update, pack 1098's switches (SPEC-0045 1098 triggers 17 to 20, R-0113; High for the gates).** Each switch now has its own success gate on a new Square press: triggers 17 and 20 need stage 31, trigger 18 flag 162 set and 167 clear, trigger 19 stage 30 (this build had used one shared guess); a failed gate shows line 18. Trigger 18's success also plays hero animation 0 after camera 7. Test: switch 18 refused with 162 clear and swapped with it set; switch 20 refused below stage 31 and swapped at 31.
- **Spec update, third batch.** **Animated-object sounds (SPEC-0031 Call-site arguments and gates, R-0111):** the registrations of packs 758 (object 2, cue 6), 1081 (objects 6 and 5, cues 5 and 6), 1247 (its 32 pairs in site order), 1249 (31, with starts 0.2 for object 12, 0.5 for 43 to 48, 0.7 for object 1), 1381 (18 with cue 7 and object 18 with cue 8) and 1398 (nine) are now made in Init over (start, 1.0); 909's pair is not, since the spec ties it to a helper chosen when the last active world is 71, which this build does not track. **Pack 886 (SPEC-0039; R-0103 part 4):** with flag 250 set Init makes no slot, thread, trigger 0 or collision mask (no set loads); with it clear the set-5 branch now calls DisableInvisibility for entities 16 to 18 (not ActivateEntity) and agro on 16 and 17. **Pack 1081's T2 (SPEC-0042):** registered on trigger 2; entering with flag 41 clear runs steps 1 to 3 (hero to locator 2, object 6, camera 5, shakes, group 1's effect, camera 4, object 4) and then, with AI 14, 13 and 12 none active (taken as all dead), the reward scene (item 103, flags 313 and 41, the weapon drawn, objects 5 and 8, the cameras), else agro on those still alive (Low for the branch order); breakable 0 is inactive with 41 set. **The Rope Gun (SPEC-0021 Item 36; R-0104 part):** the Farm's stage-6 event-50 return to locator 28 now gives item 36 when flag 6 is set (its staged scene is not reproduced). **Black Knight weak points (SPEC-0012 Open question 21):** the joints are 37, 34, 24, 9, 4 and 7 for the left knee, right knee, left under-arm, right under-arm, back and neck (this build had the knee and under-arm pairs swapped); the cylinders already ran from the joint along its frame's second axis. Tests: the full sim suite.
- **Pack 1393's guard rooms (SPEC-0044 1393; R-0090 and R-0105; High for the room structure, Medium for the rules).** New: Init initializes the seven objects per room (35), turns invisibility off for entities 0 to 7 and registers triggers 0 to 5, 7 to 19 and 21 to 24; the pack's untouched objects keep their disc state (the even gate members stay hidden). Trigger 0 runs the opening once per load (fade, room 0's guard agro, behaviour 6, home and off, hero to locator 4, the odd gate animations, cameras 8, 10, the walk to 5, camera 4, the open gate pose, camera 9, animation 81 (and 82 with the cloak), flag 697). Trigger 12 sets the area word. Entry triggers 1, 7, 13 and 19 (rooms 3 and 4 together) play the levers, select the odd gate members, close an open guard door (sound 7) and reset each guard home. Plate triggers 2, 8, 14, 23 and 24, with the gate open and the plate unlatched, open a closed guard door (mechanism, room camera, 1 s, the open form, 1 s, the normal camera) and latch. Alarms (triggers 5, 9, 15/16 and 22 while inside, and a 10-tick thread per room 0 to 3 that tests its guards for agro): sound 5, the guards agro and visible, the door closed, the even gate members, the statue pair toggled, the room camera for 2 s on the first alarm. Interpretations: "under the room's gate-state conditions" is taken as the gate word set (so an alarm runs once per entry); camera waits in the opening are taken as waiting. New host call SetAI_Behavior (`AiBehavior`): it ends script control and returns an enemy to its think start (a stand-in for the unrecovered patrol data); the host now fills the agro set each tick. Not built: the statue's turn toward the hero (no object-alignment call), triggers 3, 10 and 17 (bodies not described). Test: module_1393_rooms (Init counts, the once-only opening, the area gate, the plate latch, the thread alarm firing once, the re-armed plate). Unverified in play.
- **Spec update, locomotion (SPEC-0033 States 37 and 38, Top-edge hang, test vector 148).** State 37 phase 1 now faces out at rate 1.0; phase 2 turns with the shared 0.25 blend (with its 0.1 side push when opposed) and completes when the updated facing is within 0.99 of the inward direction (it had used rate 0.1 and the old facing), and with a ground gap of 0 or below it requests action 1 and skips the turn. The climb's top-edge hang (state 21 sub-mode 17) reads the stick only when both values are non-zero, and those values are now the camera-relative stick projected onto the panel (into the wall, and along the hero's left), so a pure-forward push is blocked only when the camera is square on. Checked and already matching: the ledge chooser's order (no climb-up fallback when the hang's reach test fails), the ladder mount (0.9, 0.8, rungs at 0.45 + 1.25k, the 1.5 gap) and the drop-test branches for states 1 to 4. Kept as before: the state-30 ground-gap gate from sub-mode 2 on (R-0106; the spec's floor-contact override is not modelled), the pull-up's forward exit at 0.95 (no event-bit 0x40 window), states 5, 6, 11 and 38 (not entered in this build). Tests: ledge_drop_test (phase 2 on the ground, the blend's rate), the climb test (vector 148). Unverified in play.
- **TriggerBaggus (SPEC-0030 row 64; R-0116; Medium-High).** New action `TriggerBaggus`: the world's records are its kind-3 type-40 groups in order (checked on the disc with the new inspector option `--groups <pack>`: pack 735's groups 3 to 7 hold locators 16 to 20, the spec's five source points, and group 20 holds templates 66 to 97 and model candidate 3, so there are six records and the sixth is unplaced). A record with a locator stands on the ground below the locator's height + 4.0. Breaking one plays cue 178 (the list has a model) and drops one item 145, the Bagguss, there; an index past the list does nothing, and the unplaced record spawns nothing here. Pack 735's sequence S1 now calls TriggerBaggus 3 to 7 after camera 10 (records 3 and 4 drop at locators 19 and 20; 5 is unplaced; 6 and 7 are out of range). Not built: the shared model's draw (the chunk-29 model-handle array's location is not given; new request R-0119), the burst 2.0 above the record, the breakable-object path. Tests: the full suites. Unverified in play.
- **The bars' loss effects (SPEC-0023 section 3, Loss response; SPEC-0034 Fixed users of group 3; R-0116 part 2; High for the rule, Medium for the projection).** New: when the health (pool) value falls and that bar's slot has no live spawner, effect 0xFA2 (0xFA3) starts at E + 1.1D + (0.5 - x)wR + (0.5 - y)hU, with (x, y) the fill's right edge and vertical centre as last drawn by the HUD (window fractions) and R, U the camera's left and up; a live slot follows its saved screen point each tick, and a fall while it is busy keeps the old anchor. The projection record's q and a terms are taken as this view's own frustum at depth 1.1 (w and h its full width and height there, so the point sits under the fill end on screen; this build's reading). **Pack 956 trigger 10 (SPEC-0043):** the note now records that targets 0 to 2 are made active with or without item 36 (the item only picks the line); SetTargetActive is still not built (no Rope Gun targets). **Roberta's shot visuals (SPEC-0036 R-0114):** the spec's step selector fails for every shipped clip (no table-backed flight or impact); this build keeps the step-0 ids as a labelled stand-in so her shots are seen, and new request R-0120 asks what the original draws. **Movies (SPEC-0008, R-0112 partial):** no change; the full decode is still open in the spec. Tests: the full suites. Unverified in play (the loss effect has not been looked at on screen).
- **Spec update review, closed (the 32 revised specs of 419e6bb).** Built across this pass: the 1324 solved scene, the reed fall's end, target points (attachment 7), counter code 5, pack 1098's switch gates, the Knight weak-point joints, R-0111 object sounds, 886 Init, 1081's T2, the Rope Gun grant, pack 1393's rooms, state 37's turn and the top-edge hang's gate, TriggerBaggus, the bars' loss effects. Checked and already matching: the ageing gate, the footprint, the desk palette, radians, the Gelert rules, the ground probe, the 1091 sequencer, the ledge chooser, the ladder mount. Still open, with their requests: blade recoil against faces (R-0118), the Bagguss model and burst (R-0119), what draws Roberta's shots (R-0120), the movie decode (R-0112, the builder grammar is still open), the 0x13 notice's visible form, 909's object-sound pair (needs the last-active-world helper), the Rope Gun itself (targets, aim and SetTargetActive), SetAI_Behavior's patrol data (taken as a calm reset), the statue turn of 1393, state 38 and the state-30 floor-contact gate (R-0106). Nothing in this pass was checked in play.
- **Headless checks of this pass's work, and one fix.** **Pack 1393** (`--play-trace --pack 1393 --trace-to`): the opening runs in order (fade, hero to locator 4, cameras 8 and 10, the walk, flag 697 on tick 344); plate 2 opens room 0's door (camera 4 from tick 561 to 680; mechanism 27 played, open form 28 on, closed form 29 off) and the hero walks through the doorway to z -700. Fix: the trace showed trigger 12 (the area word) at z -877, past rooms 0 and 1, so their entry triggers cannot run on a first pass; with the gate word read as "set by an entry" their plates would never open the doors. The word now means "closed by an alarm" (0 at load and after an entry), so plates and alarms work from the load (new test case: room 1's plate before any entry). Observed and kept (the spec's order): trigger 0 deactivates guard 0 and only an entry reactivates it, so room 0 has no live guard on a first pass and no alarm was raised. **Loss effects** (`--effects-check`, new lines): 0xFA2 and 0xFA3 resolve to definitions 264 and 263; each starts one spawner of 4 particles, gone by 60 ticks (the spec's one four-particle burst). Not looked at on screen. **Bagguss** (pack 735, trigger 21): the scene breaks records 3 and 4 on tick 521 and spawns item 145 at (3281.0, 114.5, -553.1) and (3296.9, 112.5, -537.6); the ground heights match the spec's 114.538 and 112.521. The Bagguss trace line now lists every break with its spawn result.
- **The Rope Gun (SPEC-0036 Right-stick aim mode; SPEC-0033 State 27 and Aim and anchor selection; SPEC-0030 row 126; High for the rules, stand-ins marked).** New `neopets_sim::rope`: the aim mode (starts when the right stick's Y is pushed in state 1, or in attack sub-states 1, 9 and 14 with the retained R2 choice; ends outside states 1 and 13), the anchor scan (active targets strictly within 110 of the camera eye, best dot with camera forward strictly above 0.998, a sight line from 1.5 below the eye), the rope manager (G = A - 0.935 unit(A - M), arrival at |Q - M| <= 3) and state 27's phases (0 holster; 1 face at 0.25, clip 167, fire on event bit 0x20 with cue 183, cue 184 on attach; 2 clip 168 to 0.99; 3 face at 1.0, clip 172, pull 0.8 toward G a tick, contact lets go; 4 clip 171, pull 0.15 toward the anchor 2.5 lowered while its vertical part is above 0.35, Square lets go). The host: the world's aim targets come from the type-11 n2 entries (active at load); `TargetActive` (SetTargetActive) is called by 956 (targets 0 to 2 by flag 281 on the first update, and on at trigger 10) and 1091's Init (1 to 3 off); R2 in idle with item 36 and a selected anchor enters state 27 (the spec's state-13 hop taken at once); the muzzle M = T + 0.37F - S of weapon attach frame 0; pulls go into the hero's motion step (new `extra` displacement); a world change resets the manager; R2 and Circle are now separate in the input. Drawn: a thin gold cylinder from M to the endpoint (stand-in for model key 159's repeated segments). Stand-ins (R-0121): the sight sweep tests each sampled cell's frame records against the line stopped 1.0 short; the endpoint moves 4.0 a tick and attaches on arrival; gravity is off in phases 3 and 4. Not built: the reticle and aim view marker (absent from disc), the character aim pass, cue 185, the behaviour-record and hit releases, 1096's target call (index not given). Checked headless (`NEOPETS_TRACE_ROPE=k@t`, new: aims the scan at target k, pushes the right stick at t and holds R2 from t + 5): pack 1429 target 2 from the arena floor: anchor selected on tick 202, state 27, the fire and attached clips, the reel from y 0 to 40, the hang under the anchor; Square on tick 600 lets go, the hero falls and lands on the pillar ledge (y 23.7). Pack 1091: targets 1 to 3 are off as Init sets. Tests: rope.rs (scan range and score, aim mode, the phases, contact release). Not looked at on screen.
- **Regression sweep and the playtest guide.** `scripts/sweep.py` at 600 ticks over every world entry (private/sweep_2026-10-28e.txt) after this session's work (pack 1393's rooms, the hero motion step's new `extra` displacement, the Rope Gun, the loss effects): 565 entries ok, and the 8 falls are the known ones (683, 718, 799 entry 1, 879, 985 entries 2 and 3, 1300 entry 3, 1344; R-0110). The intermittent 799 entry-0 fall did not recur. docs/PLAYTEST-ACT1.md: the 1393 guard rooms and the Rope Gun are described as built (how to use them, what is not built), and the zip-line leap (built earlier) is no longer listed as missing.
- **Developer menu chord (this build's tool, no spec; user request).** The controller's Select button no longer opens the developer menu, so it reaches the game again (the pause page, SPEC-0033 Controller map). The menu now opens and closes on a controller chord kept with the bindings (`dev menu = ...` in private/controls.txt): up to three buttons, all held, the last pressed; default LB + L3 + A. The Gamepad tab of the bindings page has a "Developer menu" row: A (or a click) records the next one to three buttons held together, kept when they are all let go; Y clears it (F1 still opens the menu); Reset restores LB + L3 + A. The chord does not fire while the bindings page is up. F1 is unchanged. Test: dev_chord_fires_and_round_trips (fires only with every button held and one newly pressed; Select alone does not; the settings text round-trips). Not tried with a controller here.
- **The stray panel in play, the developer menu marquee, and the Juppie checked (user report).** (1) The square textured panel the user saw in the middle of the Juppie arena (1217), and elsewhere, was the asset viewer's texture quad: it stood at (-3, 2, -3) in every world showing the pack's first texture, and play never hid it. It is now hidden whenever play runs (checked: a screenshot of the Act 1 Juppie fight start no longer shows it; it had been there with every placed object and every placed effect switched off). (2) Developer menu labels longer than a row (28 characters; the item and boss-fight names among them) now scroll as a marquee: the start shown for 1.5 s, then 6 characters a second, wrapping round after a gap (test long_labels_scroll). (3) The Juppie "disappearing and reappearing to attack" is its spec'd burrow: the hub plays animation 6 (submerge) and holds 7, and a stage start plays 5 (emerge) (SPEC-0015 Start of the fight, Sounds and effects); a 3000-tick trace shows it never hidden by the cell rule and never moving. New diagnostics: NEOPETS_DEV_BOSS=n (the developer menu's boss fight n, a second in, in the window), NEOPETS_TRACE_NEAR=x,z,r (placed objects near a point), NEOPETS_TRACE_QUADS=x,z,r (small scene parts near a point), NEOPETS_SKIP_FX and NEOPETS_HIDE_OBJ (placed effects and objects left out), NEOPETS_FX_IDS (more ids in --effects-check), and the placed-effect list now prints up to 40 records.
- **The Juppie's release and its hiding between slams (SPEC-0015; SPEC-0030 rows 107 and 110; user report).** The release action played animation 0 on every entity; ReleaseEntityControl requests no animation, and a boss's 0 is not an idle (the Juppie's is a death clip). At the end of intro A this made the boss play its death clip for a tick and the hub then sink it again (a second animation 6). A released boss (any of the boss controllers) now keeps its clip; townsfolk still return to 0. Traced (new NEOPETS_TRACE_JUPPIE: state, clip, progress, root and head heights per tick): after the intro the boss holds 7 until the stage start. The user's report that the Juppie vanishes between attacks matches the spec as written: the free state plays 7, which on disc is the underground hold (root -30.7), and the decision cadence keeps it there 1.5 to 3.5 s per roll (only rolling when the hero is 10 to 50 away), traced at up to 8.5 s. The user, who knows the original, says it only hides when the Tentacles spawn; that is about the original, so it is asked as R-0122 and the behaviour is unchanged until answered.
- **Shadows (SPEC-0046 Character shadow volumes; SPEC-0004 Optional per-model block; user request).** New `neopets_data::shadow` reads the type-33 shadow hulls (checked on the disc with the new `neopets-data --shadow 504`: 19 units, unit 0 exactly the spec's test vector; synthetic test reads_a_unit_with_its_lists). New `neopets_sim::shadow`: the caster choice (within 50 units, the nearest three), the light direction from a caster's light context (point lights plus the strongest directional, y held at -0.4 or below), the facing, the extrusion by 16, the silhouette walls, the caps and each polygon's count sign (tests: light_direction_rules, cube_volume_straight_down, caster_choice). The app draws the hero's volume each frame (Tor's hulls from the play outfit, Roberta's from 512; the bank index taken as the skin index) after the opaque scene, depth-tested without depth writes, as multiplies on the float picture: polygons whose outward side faces the camera multiply by f, the others by 1/f, so only surfaces inside the volume darken (the original counts the signs in a buffer and subtracts 8 where the count is positive; the multiply keeps hues, and overlapping volumes darken more than the original's single drop). The Display page has two new rows: Character shadows Off / Original (f 0.87, the drop of 8 of 255 at mid grey; the default) / Strong (0.6, this build's), and World shadows Off / On (this build's, not in the original): the solid surfaces turn lit (as with ambient occlusion), the ambient light keeps 55% of the matched brightness and a shadow-mapped sun gives the rest on faces toward it, its direction the strongest directional light of the hero's light context (else a default slant), the sky dome neither casting nor taking shadows. Character shadows keep the picture in HDR. Not built: the Gnarfas as a caster, the clip of the swept segment against the culling planes. As in the original the hero darkens inside his own hulls. Checked in screenshots (pack 1393: Original and Strong; pack 934: world shadows off and on, the trees' shadows across the path). New diagnostic NEOPETS_SHADOWS="char,world".
- **Shadow fixes (user report: blocky character shadows; world shadows often pointing the wrong way or missing).** The world-shadow sun took its direction from the hero's light context, whose strongest directional slot changes as he walks between lights (often a fill light from the side or below, clamped nearly level, which stretched the shadows out of range). It is now steady per world: the brightest directional light of the world's own light set (colour times strength; one that points level or upward is passed over), else the scene default's main light (0.666, -0.524, 0.531), held at least 0.5 downward. The original character shadow is the disc's coarse hulls (boxes per body part), so its Strong version showed them as boxes; Strong is replaced by Detailed: each character's real shape cast by the same sun's shadow map (with World shadows off only the hero, the world's characters, their items and attachments cast; the other meshes are marked as not casting, and the dome's own exclusion is kept). Original keeps the faithful volumes. Checked in screenshots: world shadows in packs 934, 1066 and 1023 (one steady direction per world), Detailed alone in 934 (the hero's own shape on the path).
- **World shadows and the plants (user report: a round shadow like a puddle left behind the moving hero, fading in about a second).** With World shadows on every plant cast, and the grass, flowers, bushes and reeds near the hero bend away from him and spring back (SPEC-0060), so their moving shadows showed as a dark patch fading behind him. Among the plants only the Tree family now casts. Not seen on screen (a still screenshot cannot show the trail).
- **No shadows from effects (user report; the user found the cause: the dust puffs of the hero's walk).** The patch behind the moving hero was the shadow of the footstep dust particles: the effect batches cast in the sun's shadow map. The effect batches (smoke, fire, dust, sparks and every other particle), the swing trails, the held-item glows, the Cloud Beast's stand-in effects, the shot markers and the rope strand are now marked as not casting. (The earlier change that keeps small plants from casting stays.)
- **Pack 950, the pit under the Farm (SPEC-0032 950; user report: the top of the vine ladder did not let the hero out).** The module was never built. Init now registers S0 on trigger 0 (a cylinder of radius 9 from y 13.3 to 24.4 round the top of the shaft); entered, it fades to black (0.5 s), posts value 30 for eW 68 and leaves to eW 5. Thread F runs once per load from Update: the long fall, camera 4 (wait), 0.5 s, the normal camera (preset 10); ReturnHeroAnimToIdle is not reproduced. Test module_950_climb_out. Traced: the fall-in lands the hero at the bottom (y -11.9); the climb itself was not traced. Also: grass, flowers and bushes cast world shadows again (the user's choice; the patch behind the moving hero was the dust puffs, fixed before).
- **1023's missing arena master and wheel host (user report; SPEC-0006 Asset-swap tables; SPEC-0045 1023).** Both are in region 1035 (Shanra at the arena, Hawker Gamzan at the wheel; entities 66 and 65), loaded by the arena district's plain zone (trigger 27). The district's cycle zone (trigger 31, a smaller sphere round the same centre) fired next and, by the slot rule as written (a plain load frees slot 0 at once), took slot 0 and unloaded 1035. The same happened in every district (27/31, 28/32, 29/33, 30/34). This build's deviation until R-0123 is answered: in set 0 (slot 1 enabled) a cycle zone takes slot 1 and the plain zone keeps slot 0. Traced from the arena: 1035 in slot 0 and 1036 in slot 1, both NPCs present. With flag 10 or 11 set (set 1) zone 0 is empty in the spec, so they are not loaded then. Test village_zones updated. New diagnostic NEOPETS_TRACE_REGIONS=a-b (the character names of regions a to b).
- **1023's arena master and the mountain flicker (user report).** (1) Shanra (entity 66) had no talk routine, so she could not be spoken to and the Meridell arena (pack 1176) could not be started. Built from SPEC-0045 1023 (arena master): by the round-result word (26, set by the arena return): 0: line 37 with flag 385 set, else the prompt of the first set of 384 to 380 (38 to 42), else 43; 1: line 44 with 385, else prompt 82; otherwise prompt 45. No: lines 52 to 56 by the same flags, else 57; yes: 46 to 50, else 51, then line 83 while the Meerca chase runs (127 set, 136 clear), otherwise the replacement load to eW 128 (pack 1176) with argument 0. Test arena_master_1023. (2) The flicker: the second world 696 repeats 1023's castle and mountain parts, and this build hides an exact repeat, but its match hashed the bounds at 0.1 precision and the two packs place the shared meshes through matrices that differ in the last digits, so the mountain copies were drawn twice and fought for depth. The match is now the same vertex count with every bound within half a unit (12 pairs between 696 and 1023, found with the new `neopets-data --parts <pack>`). Checked: two screenshots 0.05 s apart toward the mountain agree (before, patches swapped between them). Also: the second world's zone-gated parts now query the current grid's block keyed by the second world's id (SPEC-0006 Zone manager); 1023's grid has none for 696, so nothing changes there. New diagnostic NEOPETS_NO_OTHER (the second world not drawn).
- **The Meridell arena's return (SPEC-0045 1023 activate-world routine; user report: after beating the first opponent the game "teleported" him).** The arena (pack 1176) posts the result and loads 1023 again, but the village's arrival routine had no arena branch, so the hero landed at door 0 with nothing recorded: no rank flag, no line, no shield. Built: with the type-2 value for eW 90 at 10, a 0 s fade out, the hero's hit points raised to 50 percent when below (new action HeroHpAtLeast), the hero to locator 6, the result kept in the round-result word (26), equip off, 2.5 s, then Shanra's result line: a win sets the next rank (381 to 385; lines 58 to 63, 63 when holding item 106 or 107), a loss sets 380 (lines 64 to 68); both values reset. A 0.5 s fade in follows the move (this build's). Traced (NEOPETS_TRACE_KILL at tick 1100 in 1176): the win line 56, the return to 1023 at locator 6 by Shanra, line 62 ("Here's your fancy, deluxe shield"), and item 106 given and equipped. No crash in the trace. Test arena_return_1023.
- **The Neopoint steal (SPEC-0029 Layout section 4, Behavior 11a; user request: the Rogue's fighting).** The Rogue (row 153), the Thief (152) and the Thug (154) have a steal on one attack slot (Rogue and Thief: slot 2, 10 to 100 Neopoints at chance 50; Thug: slot 3, 20 to 200). The steal fields (+0x3DC minimum, +0x3EC maximum, +0x3FC selector, +0x40C chance) are now read and carried on the enemy's slots. After a landed hit of a slot with the steal bit (0x04) and selector 0: a roll in 0..=100 below the chance, the amount (the minimum when the bounds are equal, else a draw between them, swapped when reversed), at most the wallet, taken from the wallet and dropped at once as coins at the hero (coin_split). A blocked hit is taken as failing the impact gate (Open question 157). Not built: the item branch (selectors 1 to 3, the Crokabeks). New `neopets_sim::items::steal_amount` (test steal_rules). Traced in the arena (pack 1176, the Rogue, NEOPETS_TRACE_NP=500 new): slot 2 stole 68 Neopoints on tick 2219. The user's other notes on the Rogue's fighting are not known yet.
- **The Rogue's attack cadence (SPEC-0038 Behaviors 7 and 13, SPEC-0005 Playback 1a; user report: the Rogue attacked too rarely).** Checked, no behavior change: the normal choice (slots 4 to 1, strict bands, arc, per-tick chance roll, cooldowns, recovery gate), the strafe sub-state and the sequencer match the spec. The traced Rogue (pack 1176, hero standing) starts an attack within a few ticks of each recovery, but every swing plays its slot clips to their ends at R = 1.0: wind-up 48, follow-through 50 and recovery 52 are 25 keys at speed byte 2 (100 ticks each), so one hit lands about every 6 s. Asked as R-0124 (the class-17 play rate, whether E must finish, the speed byte source). New diagnostics: NEOPETS_TRACE_AGRO=1 prints each change of an enemy's outer, inner, sequencer state and slot with the distance and recovery; NEOPETS_TRACE_AGRO=clip prints the playing clip, progress and (cursor, keys, speed) every 5 ticks of an attack; `neopets-data --monster-row N` prints a monster row's class-17 values. Unverified against the original.
- **The item steal and the carried Neopoints (SPEC-0029 Behavior 11b; SPEC-0038 Behavior 34 part D).** A landed, unblocked hit of a slot with the steal bit (0x04) and selector 1, 2 or 3 now takes one held item of the selector's pair (1: 150 and 149; 2: 146 and 157; 3: 156 and 147; held ones kept in that order, one chosen uniformly; neither held: nothing). One unit leaves the inventory, the enemy carries the item with a 180-tick timer (cleared below 1), and its carried Neopoints grow by the item's catalogue price (item text table +0x0C, new `Design::catalogue_price`). On the enemy's first death tick the carried Neopoints are split into coins at the enemy (100, 25, 10, 5, 1; no spacing) after the drop placement's payout, and set to 0. On disc only rows 136 and 138 have such a slot (row 136 slot 3: selector 1, chance 100). New `neopets_sim::items::steal_item` (test item_steal_rules, the spec's vectors). Not built: the carried item drawn at 0.45 scale on the enemy. Not traced in a world (the packs that place rows 136 and 138 were not looked up).
- **1176's spectators (SPEC-0041 1176 CB1).** The encounter thread's scene now gives spectators 0 to 10 control-grabbed loops of one of animations 40, 37, 38, 55, 26, 70 and 69 each (Random(0, 6), new action RandomLoop7) before the hero is grabbed; before, they stood in their idle. Traced (pack 1176, 900 ticks): the eleven play 26, 37, 38, 40, 55, 69 and 70. Checked, already built: the 1023 fame rule (the arena talk by flags 381 to 385, which the arena return now sets, and the wheel talk by flag 480; the Random(1, 6) and Random(1, 3) draws are taken), the five arena rounds (round r by flags 381 to 384, opponent entity 12 + r, region 1179 + r) and the 1023 prize wheel; the stale module note is corrected. Not built: SetMaxActiveAI(12) and CB4, the use-item sound (main sound 142; no use-item callback hook exists yet).
- **The hero use-item hook (SPEC-0021 Use-request gate items 2 to 4a; SPEC-0030 row 269; SPEC-0041 1176 CB4 and 1371 branch A).** New `level::on_hero_use_item`: while a hook is registered every use (quick panel and item screen) is diverted: nothing is used, healed or removed, and the hook thread runs (a use while it still runs starts nothing; word 251). Hooks on disc: 1176, the Meridell arena (main sound 142), and 1371, the Coliseum, in an arena round (word 4 = 1; no host calls); both pass handled 1, so no default dispatch follows. The item screen plays the refusal cue 0xA2 for a diverted use (item 3). Test hero_use_item_hooks. Not traced in play (the use path needs a held item and an input).
- **1023's cloak watcher (SPEC-0045 1023 Update and Region loads).** With flag 11 set (Meridell occupied), a change of the tattered cloak from the state last acted on (module word 40, file value 0 = off), or the load thread's one run, starts a 5 s wait (300 ticks); when it ends with the cloak still in that state the scene runs and the word keeps the state. Put on: fade out, both cloak slots reset, j 5 (region 1029, the Dark Knight, entity 30) loaded and activated, camera 6, fade in, line 21, fade out, the slot reset, the camera returned, both slots reset, fade in. Off (or off at load): the same with line 20 ("Intruders! Attack!"), then the cloak slots load j 4 and j 3 (regions 1028 and 1027, eleven Minion Brutes and eleven Minion Wizards). The cloak slots are slots 4 and 5 here (0 to 3 are the zone loader's and the load thread's). The fade lengths are not given (this build's 0.5 s). Test cloak_watcher_1023; traced (pack 1023, --flag 11, cloak off): region 1029 on tick 331, line 20 on 362, regions 1028 and 1027 on 441, and a Minion Brute enters agro.
- **Fix, 934's low-health hint (SPEC-0045 934 thread 21).** The hint tested module word 41 (once per load) but wrote word 13, the teleport's first-time word: it showed line 214 every tick while the hero was below 0.2 of the maximum, and it suppressed the first-visit scene of teleport 0 (camera 4, hero animation 162, sound 9, objects 10 and 11). It now writes word 41. Test low_health_hint_934.
- **All-areas sweep, 2026-10-28 night (tooling; no gameplay change):** after the Rogue cadence check, the item steal, the arena spectators, the use-item hook, the cloak watcher and the 934 hint fix, `scripts/sweep.py` over 573 runs at 600 ticks as Tor: no panic, crash or NaN; 8 falls, the same known cases as the earlier 2026-10-28 sweep (transition shells 683, 718 and 799; edge walk-offs in 879, 985, 1300 and 1344, R-0110). Check.cmd passed (tests, the Bevy compile check, the source and clean-room audits).
- **1056's shield lesson (SPEC-0044 1056 Update, Shield lesson, Trainer routine).** With flag 26 and 191 clear: 188 clear auto-talks (the trainer's 187, 188, item 160 x20, lines 5 and 4, as before); with 188 set a fire shield alignment (Mote 160 or 161 in the shield word) sets 189, and once Square is released the trainer's line 3 turns the launcher on; the launcher fires LaunchFireMote from locator 101 toward 102 at 0.9, then waits 3.0 s, repeated; while 189 is set a deflected shot (a live shot among the enemy pool's first ten slots with hit selector 0, which the fire shield's reflection sets) sets 190 with the launcher off and auto-talks (191, cycle 0, 1, 2), and a shield alignment that is not fire clears 189 with the launcher off. In the shield area (trigger 9) with 188 set, 190 clear and no item 160, item 160 x20 is given. New `projectile::fire_mote` (kind 0, visual 23, fire, no owner, damage 3.0, radius 2.0, effects 528, -1, 991, -1, 991, 991, lifetime trunc(distance / speed) + 20; test fire_mote_vectors: the spec's vectors and the courtyard's 65 ticks), `Pool::deflected`, the hero view's Square-held and deflected words, and action LaunchFireMote (target: locator 102, or the hero + 1.5 up when missing). Module words 12 to 14 (launcher, countdown, step). Test shield_lesson_1056. Not traced in play (the fire shield has to be equipped through the Mote panel); the shot draws as this build's enemy-shot marker.
- **Developer menu fits the window (this build's tool; user report: a small window cut the menu off).** The menu window is laid out at its full size (it was 860 wide and clipped at 94 percent of the height) and `dev_menu::fit_window` (after UI layout) scales it down as one piece, never up, to fit inside the window with a 16-pixel margin (at least 0.35), kept at its top-left corner; Bevy's UI picking follows the transform, so clicks and hover still land on the rows. New diagnostic `NEOPETS_WINDOW=WxH`: the starting window size, kept over the display settings file (which is not written). Checked by screenshots with the menu open at 640 x 420 and 1000 x 600: every section, row and the flag detail line show.
- **Shadow polish (this build's Detailed and World shadows; user request: polish the shadows).** The sun's shadow map is 4096 texels a cascade (was Bevy's 2048) with three cascades to 200 units (the first to 20), and smaller biases (depth 0.01, normal 0.9 texels; were 0.02 and 1.8): the hero's shadow is crisp (ears, tail) and starts at the feet instead of floating off to one side. Each cascade's depth range is extended 500 units toward the sun (`extend_cascades`), so a tall caster outside a cascade's slice still reaches it. Far foliage sprites (flat cards turned to the camera) cast no shadow, and blended meshes whose vertex colours make them mostly see-through (every vertex alpha below 0.5) are marked non-casters (`mark_faint`), since Bevy's shadow pass cuts out by the texture's alpha only. The pass-1 layers (and the environment-mapped pass 0) now take the sun's light and shadow like the lit pass-0 surfaces ((1 - share) + share x N.L x shadow), so a second texture layer no longer repaints a shadowed floor at full brightness. Checked by screenshots in 1023 and 678 with Detailed and World shadows, and with characters only. Seen, not resolved: on the farm road (678) a lit wedge with straight edges remains near the camera inside the trees' shadow; it goes away when the first cascade is moved to 8 units, so it is tied to the near cascade, and it may be a gap between the cliff's and a pine's real shadows.
- **Movie cutscenes On/Off (Display page; this build's testing option, user request).** A new Display row (14 rows now) and settings key `movies` (On by default). Off passes over each queued movie at once, as a skip on its first frame would: the played word is still set and the game loop is not suspended. New diagnostic `NEOPETS_MOVIES=0/1` for a run. Traced: pack 909 with it off passes over movies 88 and 1 and the farm opening's talk shows by 6 s. The developer menu's fit-to-window scaling moved to `ui_fit::FitToWindow` and the Display page uses it too (centred, top kept). Checked by screenshots of the Display page at 1000 x 600 and 700 x 420: every row and Back show. Test settings_round_trip_and_rows covers the new row.
- **Developer menu on a controller (this build's tool; user report: reaching the second column meant scrolling through the first, and the menu's Free camera row sometimes seemed not to work).** Up and Down now move within the cursor's column and wrap; LT or RT (Tab on the keyboard) jump to the other column at the same height, and so do Left and Right on a row with no value to change (`column_step`, `other_column`; test columns). The menu's Free camera row, when it turns the camera on, closes the menu: the free camera is held still while the menu is open, so turning it on from the menu looked like nothing happened. On a controller B ends the free camera while the menu is closed. The L3 + R3 free-camera shortcut is removed (user request); F2 stays on the keyboard. The help lines and the free camera's status line say so.
- **Button pictures (Display page; this build's option, user request).** A new Display row (15 rows now) and settings key `button pictures` (playstation by default, or xbox). With Xbox, the front end's four face-button sheet assets are drawn as this build's own Xbox-style icons (a dark disc, a coloured rim and letter, anti-aliased; `button_style.rs`, test icons_are_round_and_lettered): Cross (asset 30) as a green A, Circle (32) as a red B, Square (36) as a blue X and Triangle (33) as a yellow Y, by their place on the pad. The assets were told apart by drawing every sheet asset 16 to 48 side by side (the dialogue's Square, Triangle and Circle are logical sprites 0x1B, 0x78 and 0x79, SPEC-0022; Cross was found by its picture). New `Frontend::sprite_overrides` (art drawn in place of a sheet asset at any size); a change of the choice drops the HUD's, the dialogue box's and the menus' cached sprites. New diagnostic `NEOPETS_BUTTONS=xbox`. Checked by a screenshot of the farm opening's talk: the Finish prompt shows the blue X. Not covered: button pictures drawn inside text by the font, if any.
- **F3 overlay (this build's test tool; user request: coordinates with the world number, more information, a better look, slightly see-through).** The panel is now a rounded, 55 percent see-through box with coloured section headings and a 60-frame frame-time graph (green to 60 fps, yellow to 30, red slower). Sections: Performance (fps, 1% low, average and worst frame, sim ticks, window size), Hero (X, Y, Z, facing in degrees, ground speed, the ground height under the hero and the gap to it, hero, locomotion state, HP and MP), World (pack number and location name, buffer, the second world, play tick and play time) and Scene (characters here and in all, enemies alive and fighting, enemy and hero shots, world items, region loads, entities, the camera's position and distance). It scales down with small windows like the developer menu (`ui_fit::FitToWindow` gains a right anchor). Checked by a screenshot in 1023.
- **The softlock after an arena round (SPEC-0027 Camera initialisation, SPEC-0030 row 294; user report: the game locks up after the Rogue fight).** The round's end scene in 1176 holds the hero and plays camera 5 at end mode 1 (held until a return), then loads 1023; nothing returns that camera, and this build's no-threads release of the hold never came because 1023's zone-loader thread waits there for good, so the camera froze and the hero stayed held in the village. A world load (the exterior replace and the interior entry) now ends the old world's camera play, its target, the hero hold and a scripted hero walk before the new world's Init, as the camera initialisation at every world load attaches the camera to the hero. Traced: pack 1176 round 0 (the Rogue) killed by NEOPETS_TRACE_KILL=900; the return to 1023 on tick 1385 shows no camera play and the hero free, Shanra's line 62 and the arena master's prompt follow. Unverified against the original: whether the original's hero hold survives the load (the arena return in 1023 then waits 2.5 s with the hero free in this build).
- **Developer menu long labels (this build's test tool; user request: the scrolling was too fast and moved the whole label).** A label longer than the 28-character row keeps its kind (the text up to the first ": ", such as "Item: " or "Boss fight: ") in place and scrolls only the rest in the width left; the hold before scrolling is 2 s (was 1.5) and the rate 3 characters a second (was 6). Each row's scroll restarts from the start when its label changes (a new item or boss picked), instead of following one shared clock. Test long_labels_scroll updated.
- **Level prompt choices in the dialogue box (SPEC-0022 Prompt row and Choices item 3, SPEC-0030 row 100; user report: Shanra's first talk would not continue on the Finish button).** The box drew the Square "Finish" on the last page of every text, prompts included, while a prompt's last page takes only Circle (yes) or Triangle (no), so pressing the button shown did nothing. The level dialogue box now draws, on a prompt's last page, the Circle icon with table-13 label 0x21 ("Yes") on the right and the Triangle icon with 0x22 ("No") on the left, as the memory-card boxes already did; earlier pages keep "Next". Every live LevelPrompt passes -1 for its labels, so the built-in pair is always used. Not checked by a screenshot (no headless way to open a prompt yet).
- **Cloud Beast test stage (this build's test tool and own art, not the original's; user request: a flat world to fight the cut Cloud Beast in, with what the fight needs).** The developer menu's "Cloud Beast (cut, flat test stage)" boss fight still starts a new game in 1217 with start index 2's flags and grants (the Act 3 kit), then `Play::enter_test_stage` replaces that world with `World::test_stage` (pack 90000, `play::STAGE_PACK`, which no disc pack uses): a level floor at height 0 everywhere (`World::flat`, answered by the ground probe from at or above it), one entry record at the origin facing +Z, and no geometry, objects, triggers, level script, lines or music. The loaded world's characters, threads, camera play, hold, fade and letterbox are dropped; the hero stands at the origin, and the Cloud Beast spawns on its stand-in ring of radius 16 with the hero in the middle. Drawn by the new `stage_view`: a 1200-unit floor of muted stone tiles (procedural, lit by the sun and taking shadows), the ring line and a centre mark, under sky dome 1. "Spawn the Cloud Beast here" restarts the fight on the stage; leaving is by the menu's warp, start point or another boss fight. Seen: screenshots with the boss and companion 16 units ahead, Tor with sword and shield; NEOPETS_TRACE_CLOUD shows lightning hits of 10, the fidget and the air-flow. Not checked: the hero's death and continue on the stage (its pack has no disc data to reload).
- **Spec update, shared combat rules (SPEC-0038 Layout section 6 and Behavior 13, SPEC-0005 Playback 1b, SPEC-0036 Hit detection step 4; the research room's update of 2026-10-29).** (1) Missing slot phases: `AnimData::named_clip` is the table lookup without the lookup-miss fallback (an id past the table or a clip field of 0xFFF gives none), and `HeroAnim::has`, which the enemy controllers use to test a phase, now uses it, so a phase with no clip is skipped in the same tick instead of playing clip 0. The Rogue's ids 48, 50, 51 and 52 name no clip, so its slot-3 swing is the 21-key strike alone (about 82 ticks to the end path, was about 300 with three 100-tick clip-0 cycles; R-0124 answered). (2) The charge run (sequencer state 7) now stores the slot's strike word as its play rate, as states 1, 2, 5 and 6 already did; the per-update re-time at 1.0 elsewhere was already in place. (3) The hero swing against a guarding target: a power hit is Tor's sub-state 10 with a Mote in word 2 (was sub-state 10 alone); a guarding target facing the swing (dot below its Blocking Angle) blocks it and the swing counts as landed (Tor pays its magic); a guarding target not facing it takes nothing at all and stays off the hit list, so a later window tick can still be blocked. (4) The melee-defence latch is set only while the defence hold is below 1, so a swing that starts while the hold runs is judged on its first tick after the hold ends. Checked: with the hero standing in 1176 the Rogue stays in state 8 at home (SPEC-0041 1176 arena start, already as specified). Not done yet: status factors on the enemy play rate, the unhittable bosses' own hit checks, Roberta's quick-shot payment, the arena fighters' guides (SPEC-0063) and the other changed specs.
- **Spec update, 1023 zone slots and reed angles (SPEC-0006 Slot lifetime and test vector "Pack 1023 zone slots", SPEC-0045 1023 districts, R-0123 answered; SPEC-0060 Behavior 2 step 6 and Reed angles, build guide).** Zones: plain and cycle zones now share the slot rule (slot 0 when its record is free, else slot 1 when free and enabled in set 0, else a wait for slot 0; the spec retries both each tick) and both hold their record until the hero leaves the trigger, then reset the slot (its region unloads) and free the record. The earlier deviation (cycle zones forced into slot 1) is gone: inside the arena district region 1035 (Shanra and the hawker) keeps slot 0 and the cycle region takes slot 1 (traced from (177.77, 400) in 1023: 1035 in slot 0, 1036 in slot 1). Test village_zones rewritten from the test vector. Reeds: every plant's yaw is now atan2 of two uniform [-1, 1] draws (x then z, unnormalised, so diagonals are favoured); `foliage::Reed` keeps the right row as well as up and rebuilds the frame by projecting it; each standing step runs the spring-back (latch clear and up.y below 0.999: 7.5 percent toward world up), the body contact (a sphere of radius 0.75 1.4 above the base against the hero's vertical segment of height 3.5 and radius 1.175, inside the 5-unit horizontal gate) and the bend (w from s = d - R, the target T, a jump to T from upright and a 7.5 percent ease only when already leaning further; the latch set at s at most 0.25 and cleared without contact), then the weapon test against a capsule of radius 0.5 and length 6 along the current up, only with the latch clear. The cut keeps the horizontal part of the weapon tip's motion, normalised (zero: the piece never moves off); the last fall stage now turns up 3.5 percent toward the launch and ends on |up.y| at most 0.001 (it had the blend reversed and tested the launch vector). Reeds are drawn from their frame rows; far reed sprites no longer bend or follow the fall and hide once the reed is gone. Test reed_bend_cut_fall_drop follows the spec's vectors (135-degree frame, 3 / 45 / 90 degree bends with the rebuilt rows, 49 spring-back steps from flat, the first turn (0, 0.9993, 0.0362) and 213 turns); the plain f32 sum at the origin leaves sub-state 0 on tick 10, not the spec's 11 near coordinate 1000. Seen: a screenshot among the 909 corn reeds. Not watched in motion: a cut and its fall in game.
- **Arena fighters (SPEC-0063 rounds 0 to 4; SPEC-0038 Behaviors 9, 13 and 39; SPEC-0036 Hittable predicate; user request: the arena fighters).** (1) Strike windows: the sequencer keeps the strike-window word (0xBFC): state 2 sets it while any of frame bits 0x1 to 0x10 is open; in state 3 (no C) a closed window, or an open one with bit 0x20, clears it, and an open window with the word clear goes back to state 2 with the hit list cleared, so a B clip with three windows hits three times (the Rogue's slot 3 and the Werelupe Elite's slot 2; was at most one re-entry, keyed on contact). (2) A kind-2 charge strikes only once C plays (on the tick C is requested the run clip still plays; the Henchman's and the Elite's lunges hit on the run's frames a tick after state 8). (3) An enemy is not hittable in its melee or missile dodge (agro outer 5 or 6) or, unless script-held, while animation 74, 75 or 76 plays. (4) The map search of the item types: the row's flag, chance, cooldown, type and greedy range are read (0x11C to 0x130, 0x168; `MapSearch`); each pre-step (step 6), outside outer 11 and states 16 and 18, type 0 looks for item 145 and type 1 for the coins 135 to 139 (the nearest of each id in the world pool, a roll int[1, 100] below the chance, the item on the enemy's navmesh as the route test, the hero farther than the greedy range; the last passing id wins) and sends the enemy to state 17, which runs to the item (gait 0, or the walk-home pick before any agro), gives up when the hero comes within the greedy range, and consumes it within 1.0 (the pool slot freed with no give, `ItemPool::destroy`) before falling back to its default state; types 0 and 1 never re-arm the cooldown. The Rogue and the Thief now fetch stolen coins and go home; the enemy-target types 2 to 8 are not built. Test map_search_fetches_coins. Traced in 1176 (fight route, the trace's hero at 16 HP, so each round ends on its first hits): the Rogue's charge hits at c + 36 and slot 3 at s + 16 and s + 41 (the re-entry at s + 40); round 1's lunge hits at c + 24; Sludgy's lob is made at s + 84; the Skeleton Fighter's slot 2 hits at s + 29; the Elite's wind-up gate is c + 37 and its charge hit c + 4; each lose path returns to 1023. Not seen: a third slot-3 hit, the coin fetch in game, any round won.
- **Gnarfas against its build guide (SPEC-0020 Fight AI build guide: Hero actions, What a port gets wrong; SPEC-0036 Hittable predicate and Quick-shot payment).** (1) `Character::own_hit_check` marks the bosses that set the unhittable word and run their own hit checks (Gnarfas, the Plague Serpent, the Faerie Sisters, the Black Knight, the Undead Draik); the Mote power-attack objects no longer hit them. (2) A Roberta shot that meets Gnarfas is removed and flinches him with damage 0; nothing is paid (no MP, no Mote charge); it reaches his controller as a shot (`View::shot`), so a shot meeting the missile block is only removed while a melee hit there still flinches him (the block had refused every hit). (3) The maniacal laugh plays action 0 (was 18). (4) "The hero is down" (the triumph hold, the knockback gate) is action state 16 only; it had been the busy list or the script hold, which froze him in the hold whenever the hero swung or jumped and dropped his knockbacks. Traced in 985 with the boss-fight flags: approach, orbit, melee dodge, rapid strike into exhaustion, the jump sequence and its landing slam (30), the whip and a second cycle. Not done: the rest of the guide's tick-level checks.
- **Black Knight against its build guide (SPEC-0012 G9 Weak-point loop and stun windows, G15 Hero actions).** (1) The stun gate: each stun tick the knight tests its exposed parts' gates: the knees need the hero attacking and in front (dot of the direction to the hero with the knight's forward axis above 0.25), the back attacking and behind (below -0.25), the under-arms the strike-active predicate (Tor: attack sub-state 11 or 12, or jump state 7 in air phase 8 or 9; Roberta: sub-state 5 or 12) and the hero on that arm's side (side axis +X when the knight faces +Z: the left arm above 0.25, the right below -0.25, the arm's part above 0), the neck striking and behind. A part takes a melee hit only when its gate passes (it took any body overlap before); an armed shot slot is tested whatever the gate (`View::part_shot`). A passing gate sets `Knight::exposed` for the tick. (2) The shared swing against the knight: when it is not exposed the swing is blocked whatever the facing: one block per swing (the hit list), counted as landed (Tor pays the magic and Mote charge), impact cue 34 from the weapon bank and Tor's sword bounce (sub-state 13); a power step with a Mote passes through with no effect; when exposed the swing passes through and the damage comes only from the weak-point routine (the shared hit had been subtracting from the knight's HP, which the part sum overwrote). (3) The knight is no longer a target of the shared shot test, so a shot that meets him pays nothing; the bounce and the sticky deflect return of G15 are not built (the shot passes through). Test charge_stun_and_parts extended (the knee refused without an attack and from behind, taken in front while attacking; the back from behind). Traced in 989 with the boss-fight flags: kick, approach, slow and angry swings (20 each), angry start and laugh, as before. Not seen: a stun window opened by play and a part hit in game.
- **Faerie Sisters, Plague Serpent and Undead Draik against their build guides (SPEC-0011 Hero actions; SPEC-0016 Hero actions; SPEC-0029 G11; SPEC-0036 Hittable predicate and Quick-shot payment).** (1) The shared swing resolver skips the bosses with their own hit checks for its payments: a swing hit on Gnarfas, a Faerie Sister, the Plague Serpent or the Undead Draik no longer pays Tor's magic or Mote charge, and the power step's status request is not sent to them. (2) The power step deals power 1.0 (the attack power table unused) to a Faerie Sister and to the Serpent's stun strike; Gnarfas keeps the table value (SPEC-0020). (3) A Roberta shot that meets the Serpent or the Draik pays nothing (neither is hittable). (4) The Undead Draik guards (`Draik::guarding`: every tick of stages 1 and 3 outside its stun and hit reactions, never in stage 5): the shared swing then meets it like the knight outside a stun window: blocked whatever the facing, one block per swing, counted as landed (the magic paid), cue 34 and Tor's sword bounce; a power step with a Mote passes through; exposed, the swing passes to its own check as before. Traced in 1096 (flags 305 and 35): approach, facing, claw (20) as before. Not built from these guides: the Sisters' own hit cylinders (melee radius 3.0 height 6.0, shots radius 1.5 from 3.0 up; the shared bone test stands in), the shot bounce off a guarding knight or Draik, the Serpent's shot strike using the melee damage query, and the guides' tick-level timelines.
- **Juppie and Ixi Chief against their build guides (SPEC-0015 Fight AI build guide item 11 and Hero actions; SPEC-0014 item 11, Edge cases 2 and Hero actions).** Juppie: it joins `own_hit_check` (excluded from every hero hit scan): the Mote objects no longer reach it (they had run the generic take-hit and lowered its HP), its counted stun-loop hits pay no magic or Mote charge, and it is no longer a target of the shared shot test (shots pass through its body; its mouth bounce and the Act 3 spit and dazed loop are still not built). Ixi Chief: a hero hit now counts by both routes the guide gives: its own check as before, and the shared hero hit path in every state but idle, hold and the stage transition (the block states guard: a frontal swing is blocked there and every shot bounces), the first damaging shared hit of the fight doubled; in the own check a shot's damage replaces a melee hit's in the same tick, and of several shots in one tick the last one's damage stands (they were summed). Test attacks_hits_and_charge extended (moving: 30, then 20; a frontal swing in the block: 0). Not built: the Mote power objects reaching the Ixi Chief only through the shared path (they still go to its own check).
- **Werelupe King against its build guide (SPEC-0019 G11 and G14, Hurt side).** `King::hittable` (not in the flinch, catch, roll or jump), `King::guarding` (the block, holding or recovering) and `King::hit_damage` (the first damaging hero hit of the fight doubled). The shared swing: on a King that is not hittable it does nothing; on a guarding King a swing from its front (dot of the two facings below 0) is blocked: counted as landed (Tor pays the magic), one block per swing, cue 34, Tor's sword bounce, and the King's one-shot blocked word (`King::blocked`, +0x8BC) set, which sends its block to the recovery 0x1D on its next tick; a swing from beside or behind does nothing; a power step with a Mote passes through the guard. The take-hit refuses a King that is not hittable (status ticks still hurt it in any state) and doubles the first damaging hit (it took every hit at once before). Roberta's shots no longer target a King that is not hittable or that blocks (no damage, no cost; the bounce itself is not built). Test hurt_side. Not traced in game (the fight trace does not reach the King's trigger).
- **Gelert Assassin against its build guide (SPEC-0013 G7 Hurt side).** The damage moved into the controller: a hit now takes HP only when it makes the boss flinch (not in the sword-blocked slip, the healing phases or on a clone), and the damage is `Gelert::taken`: trunc(hero hit damage x (1 + 4 x elapsed / 7200)), the factor unclamped (the elapsed counter was kept but never applied; the app had subtracted the raw damage on every hittable tick, slip and heal included). Of the hits in one tick a melee contact takes the tick, and of several shots the last one's damage stands (they were summed). Test damage_time_factor (15 at the start, 30 at 15 s, 75 at 60 s).
- **Darkest Faerie against her build guide (SPEC-0054 Hero actions and Edge cases).** She joins `own_hit_check`: the shared swing no longer pays Tor's magic or Mote charge for touching her and sends her no status (her own test takes the code-5 flinch and the form-2 hit), the Mote objects skip her, and she is no longer a target of the shared shot test, which could take a shot that met her (paying its MP) before her absorb test in code 7 saw it. Traced in 1432 (no story flags): her controller starts as before. Not checked here: the rest of her guide's timings.
- **Quick shot meets a guarding target (SPEC-0036 Arming counter, Hit on characters, Quick shot meets a guarding target; SPEC-0029 Open question 171).** Roberta's shots carry the arming counter (0 at launch; decremented each update before the move; homing and guard handling only at 0). `Character::guards` is the guard query: a blocking common enemy (outer 7 or 8), the Black Knight outside an open stun gate, the guarding Undead Draik and Werelupe King, the Ixi Chief in idle, hold, the stage transition and its blocks (Gnarfas's missile block keeps removing the shot in its own check, SPEC-0020). The shared shot test now takes a guarding target as a candidate when the shot is armed, even one that is not hittable; a quick shot that meets it bounces (`Shot::bounce`: d and the side axis negated, r1 and r2 uniform in 0 to 0.5 with a fair sign, d1 = r1 side + (1 - |r1|) d, d2 = r2 up + (1 - |r2|) d1 unnormalised, the arming counter 15), dealing no damage and paying nothing, and flies on; a full-release shot is not blocked: a hittable target takes it as before, any other is passed with the shot disarmed. Before, a blocking common enemy took the shot's damage and the guarding bosses let it through. Test shot_bounce (the three test vectors and the 15-tick arming). Not built: the deflect (the knight's and the Gelert's deflect flag turning the shot on the hero) and the second bounce's degenerate-frame quirk.
- **Juppie Act 3 (SPEC-0015 Variant: the spit flag, Spit, Spit projectile, sub-steps 5 and 6, Hit helper).** The spit flag is set at the fight's start from the Act 3 marker (story flag 0x53). With it: the chooser falls through from a failed slam roll, or a distance outside the slam band, to the spit roll (D below the spit distance 100, chance 50, a count of 3 to 5 drawn); the spit plays 0x13 turning at 0.25 with a 120-tick counter set on its request tick, then shoot cycles of 0x14 (one projectile on the first tick of the release bit 0x20; at the clip's end the cycle counts, 0x16 for that tick below the count), then 0x16 to its end and free. The projectile (`juppie::spit_setup`): pool kind 1 with gravity, visual kind 23, element 0, radius 1.0, damage 100 (the int word 0x2DAF84), from the mouth (bone 25) at the hero's feet with s = 0.5 - 0.4 (1 - (d - 5) / 95)^2 per tick, T = d / s, vy = dy / T + 0.5 (32 / 3600) T, the life trunc(60 T) + 1, effects 1400, 1400, 1399, none, 1399, 1399. A locator slam with the flag goes to the dazed entry (0x11 to its end, the 300-tick timer) and the dazed loop (animation 3, the head blocker, the HP exits). The hit helper runs every tick: a hero shot slot touching the vertical cylinder of radius 4.0 and height 6.0 on the mouth point is counted in the dazed loop when the horizontal direction from the mouth is above the dazed cone (0.9, word 0x2DAFD8) along the mouth's forward (its damage by the hero shot rule, truncated, summed, subtracted; the slot disarmed), and bounces otherwise (every contact outside the dazed loop, Act 1 included). Test spit_and_dazed_loop. Traced in 1217 with the Act 3 flags and the Tentacles killed by the developer kill: stage start, a three-cycle spit, the slam, the dazed entry and loop, back to free; with seeds 2 and 3 the spits hit the hero for 100, 48 to 50 ticks apart. Not built: the stun box blocker and the helper's deflection effects.
- **Deflected hero shots and the Juppie's stun wall (SPEC-0036 Quick shot meets a guarding target, Deflect flag, Hostile shot against the hero; SPEC-0013 state 7 and G13; SPEC-0015 sub-step 2).** Characters keep the sticky deflect flag (`Character::deflects`, +0xE5C), set on every tick of the Black Knight's parry and deflect stance and while the Gelert Assassin plays its deflect clip 21 (which also makes it guarding); nothing clears it. A quick shot that meets a guarding target with the flag is deflected (`Shot::deflect`): aimed at the hero's aim point, armed for 15 ticks, and hostile: it then steers toward the current hero and tests the hero's body cylinder (radius 1.175, height 3.5) instead of the characters; a guarding hero (state 20 or Roberta's spin) bounces it again and it stays hostile; otherwise the hero takes trunc(S x T) and the shot is disarmed, and the hit also pays Roberta's quick-shot cost (the quirk the spec keeps). The Juppie's stun (sub-step 2, not the dazed loop) now also pushes the hero out of the thin wall along its body: centre halfway from its position to its head bone at its height, 4 high, 30 along the body and 1 across. Test shot_bounce extended (the deflect). Not seen in game: a deflect by the knight or the Gelert, or the wall (the traced Act 1 run stayed in the hub).
- **Shadowglen Woods and the Act 1 Juppie fight (SPEC-0058 step 9; SPEC-0042 pack 1184; SPEC-0045 pack 1217; SPEC-0015; SPEC-0006 Locator lookup):** `neopets_sim::juppie` runs the boss (class 18): the hub (6 once, then 7) and the stage starts on level flags 1, 5 and 10 (animation 5, facing the hero), the cadence (the stage cooldown ranges, the slam between 10.0 and 50.0 at 50 in 101), the head slam (approach 0x15, slam 0x12 with the stage events tried on the window ticks until one locator of the stage is used, the head-bone impact under 7.0 for 25 raw with the 5.0 push), the stun loop (0x10 for 120 to 300 ticks, counted hits of 4 x the hero's melee damage, the count check 9 with no new draw, recover 0x11), the stage-complete marker and the stage rules (flags 3, 8 and the set-backs 0xF to 0x11), the death sequence (0xE, 10000 to the other non-townsfolk characters, HP 1) and the finale and idle holds; every tuning value is read from the boot data. `neopets_data::ground` reads the entry records' locator tags, and play looks the stage locators 101 to 106 up by tag. `neopets_sim::level` has 1184's Init (trigger 0 with line 14, the 220-dependent triggers 7, 9 or 11, flag 661, thread A's slot-B load by story set), its activate-world arrival (locators 45 and 46 with camera 10, or 50), thread B's item-73 watch and the exterior replacement to eW 130; and 1217's Init (81, 83 with 248, the fight-due test, trigger 0, region 1218 into the slot, entity 3's loop 94, the streaming filters as deactivations), its teleports (eW 68 with value 50, eW 129 with value 2), and the fight controller of Act 1 as a one-scene-at-a-time thread: intro A (cameras 4, 7, 10, 8, 6, 9, 12 and 11, line 6, the stage-1 spawn), the stage scenes (lines 7 to 9) and stage starts (level states 1, 5 and 10), the monitor (entity deaths, spawns 2 and 3 on level flags 3 and 8, the set-back respawns, the death flag 14), and the win (flags 82 and 147) with outro A (lines 10 to 13). New actions: level states, module-word bits, the exterior replacement load, and the forced bind (ActivateEntity of a dead embedded entity reads its record again and waits for it). Deactivations sent before a region loads now apply when it arrives. Not done: Act 3 (intro B, outro B, the spit and the dazed loop, the defeat check), the eleven main records, the gate and cage records and their objects, effects and sounds, PlayHeroAnim, BossFightTweakHero and the equipment display, the skip intro, the stun box blocker (the head cylinder pushes the hero out), 1184's thread C loads and E8's world-load override (R-0036). Two quirks of the rules as written: stage 1 has one usable locator, so a first stun that leaves the boss above 5/6 cannot be followed by another (R-0036); a forced bind of a living Tentacle does not add a second copy.

Step 8 (flag 145) is still blocked on item 42 (R-0029); step 10 was already built (951's thread sets 148 with 147).

- **The Miamice catch and the sneak (SPEC-0058 step 12; SPEC-0029 Behavior 28; SPEC-0033 Behaviors 1A, 4A.1, 5.1 and 18; SPEC-0005 Attach points and carrying; SPEC-0045 pack 1163):** `neopets_sim::miamouse` has the class-21 routine (the dispatch with the notice range 10, the standing-hero rule and the sneak cone 0.5403; wander with the 120 to 210-tick pause and the emote at multiples of 90; the flight with the 4-unit step and the far centroid picks of radii 10, 20 and 30; the released and landing state with the cage centroid hops, the off-mesh return home and the landing clip 19; the delivered state), the catch prompt test (below 4.0, facing dot above 0.5, catchable), the release, and the global catch switch with its meshes, count and delivery step on the level states.
