- **Hero switching (SPEC-0053 sections 2.3, 2.4, 3.1, 3.2, 3.4, 3.5, 3.6b, 3.7 table, 3.8 c, 3.9, 3.10, 3.12 and 3.13):** `neopets_sim::swap` has rule M (flags 220 and 248), the mode lock (0 Tor, 1 Roberta, 2 and 4 free, 3 and 5 blocked), the L1 and R1 chord, the switch-ready gate (state 1 or 20, on the ground, the stick at rest, not stunned, no box, the quick panel closed, no fade running), the swap task (start with the attack pools reset and the white fade over 37 ticks; the exchange once at least 3 ticks have passed and the fade has reached opaque white; the fade back over 8 ticks; the carried state, 1 for a dead or 0x12/0x13 hero; the HUD timers with the extra flag; the 41-tick hold from the truncating 0.7 x 60) and the act-start table. In play Roberta's model and animations (outfit 512) are loaded at start and parked with her start pools; the exchange swaps model, animations, pools and maxima, clears the incoming hero's status timers, takes her weapon and shield entries, sets the speaker name and colour by hero index ("Roberta" with colour slot 2), and has the renderer re-spawn the hero; the incoming hero keeps the outgoing hero's place, facing and camera. The chord is L1 (Q or the left bumper) with R1 (Shift or the right bumper); during a swap the hero takes no input. The save writes the active hero, the swap mode and both heroes' pools and maxima, and a load restores them. Not done: Roberta's attack step and shots (she cannot attack yet), the one-shot states 0x33 and their cues 0xB0 and 0xB1, the swap effects, the sound settle, the HUD portrait swing, the hero-down switch and the Potion of Revival route, the scripts' SwitchHero, ActivateHero, SetHeroSwapMode and TriggerActStart, Roberta's outfit choice, and her per-hero Mote words and quick items. Observed: after a switch Roberta stands facing the camera where Tor faced away (her model's forward axis or idle root turn is not checked).
- **Hero-down switch and the Potion of Revival (SPEC-0053 3.8 b and d; SPEC-0055 8A):** while the selected hero lies in the death state with its death clip at 0.95, a switch to the partner (both flags and the extra flag) is requested every tick; a running or accepted switch keeps the game going (the partner comes in with state 1), and a refusal or a dead partner leads on to game over as before. The Potion of Revival (item 50), in mode 2 with the partner down, heals the partner to its maximum and requests a switch to it; otherwise its use is refused (cue 0xA2). `NEOPETS_TRACE_HP` sets the hero's starting hit points for traces.
- **Script hero calls and the act start (SPEC-0030 rows 51, 56, 167, 173 and 187; SPEC-0053 3.2, 3.3, 3.5 and 3.7; SPEC-0039 module 1244):** `neopets_sim::level` gains SetHeroSwapMode, SwitchHero (a request with extra flag 0, the first flag the swap effects and the second the white flash; none for hero -1 or the selected hero), ActivateHero (MoveHero on the hero selected now, then the switch, then the block) and TriggerActStart; the hero view the scripts read now carries the swap mode, the selected hero, both heroes' death and the swap in progress. The app's `act_start` sets the mode, selects the hero (stage 1 by a request, stages 2 and 3 directly with both heroes placed at entry record 0), sets flags 220, 248 or 332 and heals the heroes in the mask. Module 1244 has its Init, the Roberta scene of trigger 0 (swap mode 2 only; it ends in mode 1), the two walk-back lines, trigger 4's scene once per level state 0, and the break scene, which ends on Tor in mode 0 with flag 272. Not reproduced: the objects, their animations, the shake and Break (R-0040), NeedHeroAlive, the sheathe sound, the swap effects, the Act 2 stash copy and Roberta's fresh kit (R-0042). 1244 is not reachable in play yet (Act 1's end, pack 1174, has no spec). Verified only by a unit test and the act-start trace below.
- **Effects: definitions, spawners, particles and sprites (SPEC-0034 Where it lives, Layout 2.1 to 2.8, Behavior 3.1 to 3.4, 3.16, 4.1 to 4.7, 5.2, section 12's series; SPEC-0029 Behavior 9 effect ids and the shared hit flash; SPEC-0015 head slam):** `neopets_data::effect` finds resource 9's type-34 chunk and decodes the 123 shader-list groups with their blend (the GS ALPHA register's B field; 46 additive, as the spec counts). `neopets_sim::effect` parses the table (666 definitions, 1,651 nodes, A, B and C records; id lookups 101 -> 211, 159 -> 542, 1201 -> 161, 938 -> 403, 4007 -> 496 and 162 none, as the spec gives), and runs effect objects (cull, cone, near/far state, the emitter gate and the kept handles), the spawner clock in single precision, drain, birth (ring, life, cone direction through the folded series, speed, spin, acceleration), motion (drag, kicks), the loop restart and the colour and size curves. The app ticks the shared pools every play tick and draws live sprite particles as camera-facing quads, one mesh per material group with its texture and blend. Users: hero footstep dust (the 28-id table at 0x3150A8 by ground code: 163 dirt, 181 grass, none for water, muck and wood), Juppie's head-slam flash 222, and the six projectile effect ids (monster row +0x46C): flight effects 0 and 1 as objects on the shot, and blocked, expiry, world-impact and hit effects. `--effects-check` reproduces test vectors 70 (positions, velocities, the kick on tick 32, bursts on ticks 1, 8, 14, 20, population 400) and 71 (bytes 95,95,95,24; end on tick 361; spawner dead by 362) exactly; vector 72's restart bytes come out 63,127,127,18 where the spec gives 64,129,129,18 (R-0043). Not covered: model particles (no child models drawn), light emitters, the clip effect lists of characters (type 36), placed effects (type 39) and StartEffect, swing trails, the pulse, presets, status emitters, the per-character and per-world pools and the hero attack-family effects. The two birth perpendiculars, the cosine through the series, the footstep effect's frame and the sprite UV orientation are this build's choices (R-0043).
- **Clip effect lists (SPEC-0034 Layout 1, 1.1, 1.2, 5.1; SPEC-0006 kind 16):** `neopets_data::region` reads the kind-16 effect lists of a region's type-36 table (entries with both points, attach slots with the 0x4FFFFFFF sentinel, start and end fractions, id, ground flags, positional mode, trail and full-frame bytes). Each region character's animation carries its region's lists; the clip's list is word A bits 14 to 21 of its main table entry. Every play tick each character's entries start their effect while the layer-0 cursor is in [s, s + L) (s = start x D, or L for start 0) and stop it in [e, e + L) (e = end x D, at most D - 1); a new list stops the old list's effects, and running effects follow point 1. Point 1 is attach slot A's frame applied to the entry's offset, then the owner-ground (+0x3D) or 8.0 probe (+0x3C) rule; +0x49 passes the whole frame. Verified by trace: Juppie starts 480 twice on actions 5 and 6 and the mud splash 474 on 17 and 18, as SPEC-0015 lists, and the Tentacles start 1435, 1437 and 1438. Unverified: matrix slot k is taken as attach point k, list-started objects are updated once at start and then only moved, and positional entries also start their id as an effect (R-0044). Not covered: swing trails (+0x48 = 0), the positional sound, shake and rumble, and the hero's own clips (the hero tables carry the field; the lists of a hero are not located).
- **Save-point glow (SPEC-0034 section 36; SPEC-0025 Save points step 2):** the current world's region owns effect objects of definitions 400 and 404; when a branch's nearest save point changes they are stopped, moved there and started again, every tick they are updated with the camera as the listener (near within 25 units, far beyond), and leaving the world stops them. Screenshot in 678 (`--play --pack 678 --look -1706,1193`): 404's far sparks and disc over the pedestal.
- **Placed effects and the effect script calls (SPEC-0034 section 6; SPEC-0028 sections 4 and 9; SPEC-0030 rows 5 and 6; SPEC-0045 1098 Update and 909 Act 1 start):** `neopets_data::effect::placed` reads a world pack's type-39 records (frame rows, position, id, zone, cull). StartEffect(slot, camera) binds record `slot` of the thread's world as an effect object (the record's own cull kept when positive or -1) and runs one update, with the camera as the listener when the flag is set; StopEffect drains it; a world that is no longer resident drains and releases its started records. Module 1098's Update starts effects 0 to 2 each tick while flag 642 is clear (stops them while it is set) and effect 3 while 642 is set and 632 clear. Module 909's Act 1 start now makes the act-start 1 call. The trace prints a world's placed records (1098: 136, slots 0 to 3 are id 792). Not covered: the active flag, the type-40 switch path and the zone gate (no zone grids yet), MoveEffect and its variants, and the start's camera frame where a module does not give the flag (1098 passes none here).
- **Roberta's hold effect and leap glow (SPEC-0036 Roberta full-charge hold effect, Timed glow):** `neopets_sim::roberta::Displays` keeps the two single-precision timers: effect 239 shows from the 49th pass in sub-state 1 with F at least 80 (started, then updated at the hero's frame each pass), effect 0xF1 shows from the 31st through the 48th tick of sub-state 8 at the hero + (0, 0.5, 0) on its flat facing; any other state stops and resets them. Unit-tested to the spec's vectors. Not seen in a trace yet: the fight trace's held attack releases at F = 90 before 49 qualifying passes. Note for traces: with flag 707 on and flag 1 off, 909's Update now makes the act-start 1 call (swap mode 0), so switching traces that start in 909 need `--flag 1`.
- **Status display: effects and tint (SPEC-0036 Status display and cues; SPEC-0034 5.4):** `neopets_sim::status::Display` keeps the shown status and its 240-tick rotation over codes 0 to 7 (a shown status that ends gives way to the next active one, or none); `display_effect` gives the ids (0xCC stun, 0xBE curse, 0xC8 poison, 0xCB slow, 0xBF strength, 0xC0 speed, 0xC9 confusion, 0xCA fire; one-shots 0xC2 to 0xC4 for codes 8 to 10, 0xCD when a stun ends); `tint` gives the steady tint with each factor capped at 1.0 and cleared when all are 1.0 (fire shows none). The hero and every live common enemy have a display: a status that becomes active runs the display start, the shown effect follows the character (curse 1.5 above), and the renderer multiplies the character's vertex colours by the tint. Screenshot (`NEOPETS_TRACE_STATUS="2,60" --play --pack 678`): Tor tinted green with the poison bubbles. Unverified: the effects sit at the character's origin, because the main attach point (and fire's two other points) are not identified; a re-apply of an already active status does not restart the display; the stun start's flash colour (0, 0.5, 1.7) is not drawn (R-0045). Superseded by the next bullet for the tint.
- **Colour-override slot 0 (SPEC-0046 draw step 6, Colour-override slots; SPEC-0034 5.4 tint step and section 21):** `neopets_sim::status::Override` holds slot 0 (on, reference, mode, counter, stored colour): the set starts the counter at 0 in mode 1 and 30 otherwise only when the slot was off, mode 1 counts up each tick, and while on the stored colour becomes (1 - f) x itself + f x the reference with f the 60-tick triangle; `triangle` gives SPEC-0046's vectors (0, 0.5, 1, 0.5, 0); 1 test. Each tick the status display clears the slot when the shown status changes, the stun start sets (0, 0.5, 1.7) in mode 0 (uncapped, so it brightens blue), and the tint step of codes 1 to 5 and 7 sets the capped colour in mode 1 (clearing it for white). Region characters get the per-tick colour push (white here: the 15 colour words' draw mapping is R-0027) before the blend, so an enemy pulses toward the tint and back once a second; the hero has no push, so its stored colour accumulates and settles on the tint (trace `NEOPETS_TRACE_STATUS="2,3"`: (0.206, 1, 0.603) on tick 75, then (0.2, 1, 0.6); stun: (0, 0.5, 1.7) from tick 75). The renderer multiplies the vertex colours by the stored colour. Deviation: the hero's stored colour is reset to white when the slot is cleared, because read literally it would keep the tint after the status ends (R-0052).
- **Foliage patches drawn (SPEC-0060 Layout, Behavior 1 to 3; SPEC-0006 Box records):** `neopets_data::foliage` reads every type-16 chunk (records, rows and plants by their header-relative offsets; pack 678 gives SPEC-0006's check values: 71 patches, record 0 key 9, height 74.1661, rows (5, 2.073) (2, 3.0792) (1, 4.4705) (1, 0), plant offset 1.2959). `neopets_sim::foliage` has the key table (six families with their near and far registry ids, the snap rule and the scale range), the jitter rule, the bush probe ceiling of 8, the far distance and the placement (jitter, ground snap with no plant where there is no ground, random yaw, scale in [s0, 1] or [1, 3.5] for model 265). The app builds each resident world's patches as a world part: the family's near model (else its far model) from the pack's type-8 children by registry id (283 to 320 from resource 9), one entity per plant and part with a material per plant colour (128 = full). Screenshots: trees, bushes and grass along the 678 road and the 909 farm (550 and 805 plants). Not covered: the far distance cut and the near/far fades and far batches (the LOD node's distances are not identified), the family logic (grass sway and fade, bush cutting, drops, regrowth and sparkle 139, key 4's trunk, reed bending and the cut-piece fall) and the once-per-area gates of the clover and Negg patches (R-0046).
- **Bush cutting, regrowth, drops and sparkle (SPEC-0060 Behavior 1, 6 and 7):** the play now builds each resident world's patches (World keeps the type-16 patches and its type-8 children's registry ids; a family whose model the pack does not register builds nothing) and drops a world's patches when it leaves. Bushes run the state machine of `neopets_sim::foliage::Bush` while the camera eye is within 100 units of the patch: the weapon test within 5 units of the hero during the attack window (the hero weapon segment against a capsule of radius 0.5 and length 6.0 up from the bush base), the shot test (each of Roberta's shots within its radius + 1.5 of the base; never for key 5), every trigger in a step cuts again; the cut shrinks the bush over 3,800 ticks and it regrows over 600 (cannot be cut while the regrow counter is 450 or less); each cut rolls a drop by the main model (the default coin rule, the item rules, the coin bush's four coins with luck, the Negg bushes while their once-per-area pair is clear) and spawns it through the item route; a grown coin bush or open Negg bush starts effect 139 every 18 to 42 ticks. The renderer draws a bush at its draw scale and swaps in its cut model in state 0. Trace: a cut on the 678 road dropped item 136 (5 NP). Not covered: the hit cues 20 and 28 (their bank is not given), the debris pieces, the bend and spring-back, key 5's contact helper and the effect's camera-facing frame.
- **Tree trunks (SPEC-0060 Behavior 8):** in the current world, each key-4 tree patch within 100 units of the camera eye tests every tree's solid cylinder (height 25, radius 5.5 x the tree's scale) against the hero, as a horizontal push of the hero's body out of the cylinder (the shared cylinder contact of SPEC-0018 is not built). Trace in 909: a hero started inside the trunk at (-1831.96, 61.55, 1026.88) (radius 4.94) is pushed out on tick 1. The trace header lists a world's key-4 tree patches. Not covered: the near-tree list for the camera's collision, and a key-4 patch whose near model is absent (it builds no trunk here).
- **Hurting surface and the deadly-ground death effects (SPEC-0057 7A rules 1 and 2; SPEC-0055 sections 8A and 9; SPEC-0034 section 37):** a hero on a non-deadly ground entry with surface word 2 and a gap below 0.2 takes a normal 10 HP hit (no knockback) on each tick where clock - trunc(clock x 0.4) x 2.5 < dt, so once every 2.5 s of game clock (the clock is the play tick count / 60); a hero who enters the death state on deadly ground gets the surface's one-shot (0x81 hot burst for surface 2, 0xBC water splash for 8) at the hero. Unit-tested to TV-G1 to G3 and G5; not seen in a trace (the 23 hurting entries are in packs 696, 702, 714, 753, 864, 879 and 1080). Not covered: rule 3 (the linked overlay's point test, the deep-overlay kill of both heroes and the overlay's hurting tick), because the ground reader does not expose the overlay entry's point test and height.
- **Animated placed objects (SPEC-0028 sections 7, 7.1 and 10; SPEC-0030 rows 1 to 4, 9 and 11; SPEC-0045 909 object calls):** `neopets_data::world::objects` reads each type-29 object (inline mesh tree, rest block, scale, state, key count and motion stream). `neopets_sim::object` parses the ten-channel stream (constant or F samples, s16 / 1024, keys at 15 per second), samples it with the absolute rule (rotation lerp with the sign flip, the summed translation changes plus f of the next, scale lerp; the per-tick translation path needs a locator link, which is not built) and runs the script states (InitObjOn and ActivateObj to state 9 or 8, PlayAnim state 2 with a negative speed starting at the end, LoopAnim state 0 with the wrap, StopAnim state 10 unless finished, DeactivateObj state 4) and the per-tick update of states 0, 2, 5 and 6. Level scripts gain InitObjOn, PlayAnim (with WAIT the thread waits while the object plays once), LoopAnim, StopAnim and DeactivateObj. The world scene no longer bakes objects in: each object is its own entity whose transform comes from the play each frame (R(q) x scale at root + rest translation). Module 909 now makes its object calls (the Init object helper by flags 122 and 110, the flag-110 and flag-122 steps, the flag-117 scenes, the pump exchange and the stage-1 scene's object 8), and its objects follow the draw gate (hidden until initialised); in packs whose object calls are not built yet, untouched objects are drawn at rest (a deviation, so scenes do not lose their objects). The trace prints the scripted objects' states: in 909 with flags 110 and 122 at load, objects 10, 11, 15 and 16 start finished and 2 and 5 at their start, as Init says. `--start x,z[,y]` now also works in window mode. Not covered: the script offset and extra rotation, locator links, MoveObj calls, the objects' moving walls and riders, AddAnimObjSound, IsObjAnimating and the other queries in modules.
- **More module object calls (SPEC-0041 951 Init step 4; SPEC-0042 678 fight-watch thread; SPEC-0045 1163 Init):** 951 initialises object 0 at its start (end with flag 121); the 678 road fight activates objects 0 and 1 when the fight starts and deactivates them before the end scene; 1163 initialises object 0 with its trigger. The draw gate now applies in 909, 951, 678/686/691, 1023, 1244, 1133, 1148, 1150, 1172, 1184, 1056 and 1163 (modules whose object calls are all built, or that make none), except that an object a type-40 group names stays drawn at rest until a script touches it, since the group setups (chests and the other kinds) are not built. Trace counts (objects in all, drawn): 909 23/15, 951 2/2, 678 2/0 before the fight, 1184 32/32, 1056 17/6, 1163 1/1, 1172 32/32. Module 1098's twelve Init object calls are not listed in the spec, so 1098 keeps drawing its objects at rest (R-0048).
- **1023 and 1244 object calls (SPEC-0045 1023 chase cutscene; SPEC-0039 1244 Init and trigger 3):** the 1023 chase start activates object 4 and plays it (speed 0.45, wait) and deactivates it before line 27 (its reset to time 0 at locator 498, a MoveObj call, is not built); 1023 draws 2 of its 6 objects at load (the wheel and object 5). 1244's Init sets objects 5, 0, 1 and 4 off with flag 273 clear, and its break scene plays object 5 (wait), turns objects 5, 4 and 1 off and plays object 6.
- **The 1217 object records (SPEC-0045 1217 Main records, Gate records, Cage records, thread routine 2, intro A, outro A and the fight controller's requests):** `neopets_sim::burrows` holds the eleven main records (rise A, idle B and hit C groups of nine objects, effect E = 10 + r, and the inner record with P, Q, both R, the 13 S objects and T; the outer states 1 to 5 and inner states 1 to 7 with the stamp 0.4 s after inner state 4), the two gates (27-object A and B groups, the single object, loop sounds 8 and 9) and the Act 1 cage (7-object groups, entity 3, extra object 554), all stepped every pass with the request rule. Init fills them on the fight-due branch (effects 10 to 23 stopped, object 554 at its start, the cage's request 2 of thread routine 12); intro A requests state 1 on both gates and 2 on the cage and starts thread routine 2 (request 1 to the main records, Random(10, 20) ticks apart); the monitor gives the set-back inner requests (level flags 15 to 17), the closes (level 4 record 0, level 9 records 1 and 2), the slams (level flags 2, 6, 7, 11 to 13 and 8 for records 1 and 2), the hits (HasHitEntity in state 2) and the death steps (inner request 4), and a stage now completes when its records are all in inner state 6; outro A closes the eleven records 15 ticks apart, then gate 1 and the cage (with the wait while the cage is in state 4). In 1217 the objects the records name follow the draw gate; objects 310 to 363 (switched by the helper routines, not listed one by one) are drawn at rest. Trace: intro A raises the records, line 7 after the stage-1 death step, and `NEOPETS_TRACE_LEVEL="14@3000"` runs outro A with the records closed (drawn objects 313 then 117) and lines 10 to 13. Effects 23, 21 and 22 are moved to locators 55 + 3r, 56 + 3r and 57 + 3r (MoveEffect, SPEC-0030 row 28, point form: the placed effect takes the entry record's position) before they start. Not covered: the Act 3 records and the skip intro. Note: the stagger's draws come from the shared generator, so the fight trace's course changes with them (the hero now takes a head slam in stage 1).
- **Lock chest objects and the open sequence (SPEC-0048 Lock chests set-up and press rule steps 1, 5 to 7):** each world's Init now also runs the kind-1 group set-up: the chest object (the group's object 0) at its start, or finished at its last frame when its collected entry is set. The gate tests the chest object (drawn, not animating, not finished), and the open is a script thread: camera 0 of the group, the hero to locator 0, the chest's open clip (on and played at 1.0), effect 790 at X + 2.9 d facing d, cue 142, hero animation 162 (waited through), the reward (the drop on reward [0] at X + 2.4 d + 0.4 up and the collected entry), a 1 s wait, camera 1, a 1.5 s wait and the normal camera. Trace in 951 (`--play-trace 600 --pack 951 --give 15 --start "-14,-21,14"`): the chest object plays (state 2), effect 790's spawners run and the Wooden Sword drops and is picked up. Not covered: reward [1]'s character activation and the kind-2 sealed chests' objects.
- **PlayHeroAnim (SPEC-0030 row 157; SPEC-0040 Behavior 15.3 route 51):** the hero's one-shot state 51 plays the requested animation (the clip restarted) and at progress 0.99 returns to state 1 with CTL set (33 without; the scripts' CTL is taken as 1, R-0049); the call always yields one tick and with WAIT yields while the hero stays in state 51. So the scripts' hero animations now play (the chest's 162, 1244's 125 and the others). Trace: the 951 chest open holds state 51 for 77 ticks before the reward.
- **Reaction objects (SPEC-0048 Reaction object, type-40 kind 5):** the group set-up turns object 0 on and loops it at 1.0; a first fire of trigger 0 while object 1 is not drawn runs the reaction as a thread (object 0 hidden, object 1 shown, played once and waited for, set to time 0 and hidden, object 0 shown and looped again), so a fire during a run starts nothing. The trace header lists a world's script groups. Not seen in a trace: none of the packs checked (909, 1023, 1184, 1056, 1098, 1172, 1217, 951, 678, 691, 1163, 1150, 1225, 696) has a kind-5 group.
- **Sewer module 1171 and script-group types (SPEC-0042 1171; SPEC-0048 Script groups, kind 12 and type registration; SPEC-0030 row 10):** RegisterScriptGroupType is a host call: every entry of the kind gets its first trigger routed to the run callback with the entry as owner (replacing an earlier RegisterTrigger on that index, and replaced by a later one) and its init callback at once. 1171's Init turns its ten objects off, registers the levers on triggers 5 to 7, T0 and T1, the switch handler on 29 to 32 and then group type 12, so 29 to 32 run the floating-object routine R and never the switch handler. Init G: A off, its time Random(0, 100) / 100 (SetObjToTime: clamped, stopped below the end, finished at it), looped at 1.0. Run R on the entering fire: A off, B shown and played with wait, a wait while the trigger stays non-zero, B off, C at time 0 played with wait, C off, A looped again. Levers L5 to L7 (objects 11/12, 13/14, 15/16; walk points 56 to 58; tracks 4, 7, 6; sounds 56 to 58; gates 2, 0, 1): the Square prompt (6) while inside, then the walk and orient, the lever objects, hero animation 140, the fades, the camera, the looping sound, the gate object's animation, 1 s, the kill, the normal camera, once per load. T0 counts the cleared clogs (flags 637 to 641, setting 162 + count) and says line count below 5; T1 replaces the exterior with eW 126. 1 test. Traces: 34 floating objects loop from the load; standing in trigger 30 hides 20 and plays 21 to its end and waits; walking through it runs the whole cycle and 20 loops again (the spec's vector: R for entry 4, not the switch handler); lever L5 walks, fades, plays camera 4 and opens gate object 2. Not built here (the drains, their Update passes and the drain-flag loop were added later): AddAnimObjSound, Update's object pass (it re-loops objects that G already looped), and T1's blackboard value (its message type is not given, R-0053). Clean side: a lever's word is set when its sequence starts rather than at its end, so it cannot be started twice.
- **Module 880 (SPEC-0039 880; SPEC-0030 rows 45 and 261):** Init turns objects 0 and 1 off, registers handler 1 on triggers 5 to 7 (Square context 3; lines 21, 22, 24), handler 2 on 0 (entity 3's line once per pass of trigger 1 then 0, by the flag-67 rotation 0 to 2 or 3 to 5), handler 3 on 1 (clears the talked word), handler 9 on 2 (line 25 while 252 is clear) and handler 5 on the kind-7 group 2's triggers (RegisterScriptInstanceTrigger is now a host call: a kind-7 entry registers all its member triggers); thread 4 loads slot 0 with set 3, 2, 1 or 0 by flags 52, 51, 50, 49 (region 881 + set). Handler 5 on entering: with 296 clear slot 0 is emptied and slot 1 loads set 4; after the hero leaves, slot 1 is emptied and thread 4 reloads slot 0. The map shop on entities 3 and 7 offers the first map not held of 1, 8, 30, 27, 18, 3, 6, 11 with its prompt and price (250 to 400), declines with 16, refuses with 15, sells with item 175 and line 14, and rotates 11 to 13 when all are held. Teleport 0 (context 13) is shut with line 20 until 296, then sends value 80 for eW 81 and replaces the exterior with eW 81; the activate-world callback places the hero at locator 2 for 80. Update sets 296 when AI 21, 22 and 23 are dead. IsAI_Dead: the host fills the level state each tick with the current world's entity ids whose bound character is dead or dying, or that died earlier in this load (the kill memory is kept per resident world). 1 test. Trace (`NEOPETS_NEOPOINTS=1000`, following Gord the Merchant): slot 0 loads region 881, trigger 0 gives Gord's line 3, then maps 1, 8 and 30 are bought (prompts 6 to 8, line 14 each) and map 27 (300) is refused with line 15 on the remaining 250. Not built: SetWorldCollisMask, the debug lines, the kill bits of unbound records (SPEC-0006 kill memory).
- **Module 1230 (SPEC-0039 1230):** Init registers handler 2 on triggers 0, 2 and 1 (Square context 3; lines 18, 19, 20), handler 3 on 5 (line 21 while 64 is set and 248 clear), and with flag 20 or 19 handlers 5 and 6 on 3 and 4 (entity 46's flag-67 line rotation, once per pass, as in 880), and turns object 0 off with 64 set and 248 clear; Update deactivates object 0 otherwise. Thread 1 loads slot 0 with set 6 under 248, else set 3, 2 or 1 by flags 19, 18, 52, else set 0 (region 1231 + set). The map shop (shared with 880) on entities 46 and 84 offers maps 1, 8, 30, 27, 18 (500) and 10. 1 test. Trace: slot 0 loads region 1231 (Crokabeks, thieves, bandits, skeletons). Not built: flag 20's branch of thread 1 (it chooses set 5 or 4 by two flags the spec does not name, R-0054; nothing is loaded then), the extra SlotCreate and SlotLoad in that routine, BlockPlayerControl and ReturnHeroAnimToIdle in handler 3, and the collision and display masks under 248.
- **Module 886 (SPEC-0039 886):** Init registers handler 1 on trigger 5 (line 6 while 250 is clear), 2 on 2 (Square context 3, line 7) and 4 on 0 (line 8 while 252 is clear), turns object 3 off while 250 is clear, sets 249 under 248 (movie 30 counts as not played: movies are not built), and thread 3 loads slot 0 with set 3, 2, 1 or 0 by the first set flag of 52, 51, 50, 49 (region 887 + set; read as a first-match chain), nothing otherwise. Update: the hint helper's 300-tick counter, then line 1 once unless Roberta is active, then line 3 once after 2 s with Roberta active, AI 16 and 17 dead and 18 alive; the end helper with AI 16 to 18 dead turns object 3 off, sets 250, waits 6 s and fades out and in (movie 32 is not built). 1 test. Trace: line 1 ("Press both L1 button and R1 button to switch to Roberta and shoot those flying minions.") on tick 301; with flag 50 slot 0 loads region 888 (a rogue and two thugs). Not built: SetWorldCollisMask, PlayFMV 32, the debug lines.
- **Module 991 (SPEC-0045 991):** Init sets 85, registers trigger routine A on triggers 4 and 5 and on the kind-7 group 0's trigger (10), and thread 2 loads slot 0 with region 995 (flag 86) or 1000. Routine A on an entering fire picks the crowd set (2 with 86 clear; 1 with 86, 299, 414 set and 417 clear; else 0) and the zone's region (996/998/999, 997/998/999, 1001/1002/none), and thread 4 waits until the crowd record (a module word) is free, marks it, resets slot 1 and loads the region, waits until the hero leaves that trigger, resets the slot and frees the record (new waits: a module word, a trigger record). The proximity routines: the Tor and Roberta line triples of entities 4 to 8, 10/13, 11, 18, 19 and 20, the flag-86 pairs or triples of 9/12/40, 15/42 and 16/41, entity 14's item-12 ask (lines 10, 7, 8, 9, 6; flags 416, 417), entities 1, 2 and 3's line triples and prompts (667 is never set), and entity 17 the map seller (counter C: line 89, prompts 90 to 92 for maps 21, 17 and 35 at 300, 350 and 400, held maps skipped, lines 97 to 103, line 93 resetting C). Teleports 0 to 7 (context 13) post 10 to 80 for eW 81 (and for eW 88 on 4 and 5) and enter eW 84 to 87 and 89 or replace the exterior with eW 88 or eW 63; the activate-world callback places the hero at locator value / 10 + 2. 1 test. Traces: Init loads region 995 (five guardsmen) with flag 86; with set 1 starting in trigger 5 loads 998 (Steen, Lady Marissa, Monty) and starting in trigger 4 loads 997 (Sir Jaceus, Ironscale, Prince Tourin), the spec's regions; the leave-and-reset half is covered by the test only (straight walks out of trigger 5 hit walls). Deviations: the slots are numbered 0 (thread 2) and 1 (crowds) here, as the spec does not resolve them; entities 1 to 3 show one line of their triple per talk followed by the prompt, and entity 3's yes line is 165 (the spec's two descriptions differ: R-0055). Not built: the dormant villager controller (thread 3).

- **Shared entity-control routine (SPEC-0043, Shared entity-control routine):** `neopets_sim::entityctl` holds the table (at most nine records) with Add (its idle loop started), Add animation, Add point and Set active, and the per-tick steps 1 to 7: talking entities frozen, the two countdowns, a walking entity skipped, the move step (move clock against move delay; the next point in a cycle; a Random dwell delay when the point dwells; WalkEntityAndOrient or WalkEntity, stop 1.1), the clocks on idle ticks, the animation step (the point's animation or a Random pick, PlayEntityAnim for -1 seconds as 1.5 s, else LoopEntityAnim, the timer in ticks) and the idle loop asked for again on idle ticks (the host does not restart a clip already playing). `level::entity_setup` fills the table at each Init from the spec's records for 750, 1008, 1011, 1014, 1020, and under their flags 1409, 1414, 1421 and 1423, and the play steps it every tick after the module's Update with IsEntityTalking (the talk partner while a box is open) and IsEntityWalkToPointActive (a script walk). 2 tests (the spec's 745 vector: the first move on the tenth idle tick to point 9, then 7, 8, 9, the dwell, only listed animations; talking freezes; the nine-record limit). Trace: in 1414 with flag 102, entities 7, 8 and 9 start their first walks on tick 11 and walk again after their dwell (ticks 881 to 1042). Not set up: 909, 951, 1098, 1150 and 1163 (their records follow story stages or are parked around cutscenes), and the modules not built yet (722, 745, 748, 749, 752, 1173, 1405, 1407, 1411, 1417, 1419, 1425); the anim delay is drawn at the first step rather than at the add (the same values, one tick's order apart).

- **The countdown timer and the 758 time trial (SPEC-0023 section 31; SPEC-0030 rows 244 to 249; SPEC-0059 section 5):** `neopets_sim::timer` holds the engine-wide timer (visible, running, value, limit, direction): Activate (no clamp), Deactivate, and the per-tick step of 1/60 s with the limit as a floor or cap (a negative limit is none; a stopped or hidden timer neither steps nor clamps); it steps in play and not while a dialogue box is up; 1 test (the spec's clamp rules). ActivateTimer and DeactivateTimer are thread calls. The 758 keeper (entities 26 and 28) offers the run by the ladder flags (147 talk only with 435; prompts 148, 149, 150; no 158; yes 159 and the start: the clock held at 0, the fade, the course objects 6 and 3, the hero to locator 150, line 157, armed, the clock counting up to 600 s); the finish gate (trigger 15) plays object 2, freezes the clock at T (shown counting down to T) and plays objects 6 and 3, then object 2 back once the hero leaves; the judge (trigger 14) walks the hero to locator 148 and sets 431, 433 or 435 for a whole-second time of at most 180, 90 or 45 (lines 151 to 156), resets the course and turns the clock off. 1 test (the ladder: 100 s sets 431, 100 s then fails, 60.9 s sets 433, 45.99 s sets 435). The trace prints the clock while it is shown. The clock is drawn as element 0xD8 (SPEC-0023 section 13): M:SS:CC from the hundredths rounded half away from zero and clamped to 59 999 (a negative value shows 9:59:99), right-aligned at x 436 with its baseline at y 77, in the element's value-text font and the default colour; screenshot with `NEOPETS_TRACE_TIMER=125.5` (a diagnostic clock from tick 60): 2:00:65 at the top right; 1 test for the text. The timer's colour word (Activate's default, 934's red blink below 10 s: (220, 40, 40, 128) when floor(2t) is odd) colours the text. Not built: the counter element 0xD9, the hint trigger 13 (its line choice by flags 433 and 431 is not given) and the breakables 17 to 20 of the course reset (added later).
- **Module 934, Farm Road and the find game (SPEC-0045 934; SPEC-0059 section 6):** Init sets 276 under its gate, initialises objects 10 and 11, registers trigger routines 16 (7 to 9: Square context 3, lines 208 to 210) and 17 (3 to 5: the quit prompt while a game runs), the area threads 9 to 11 (here as routines on triggers 0 to 2: wait for the lock, load slot 0 with the area's set by flags 248, 2, 145, 4 and 5, lock, wait until the hero leaves, reset, unlock) and thread 12's load of slot 1 (region 940 or 946). The one-target find game: the offer (lines 30 to 33 with T = 300, 240 or 180 by flags 471 to 473; prompt 34; no 61), the start (a fade, entity 9 hidden at locator 3 + Random(0, 7), animation 42 looped, the clock counting down from T, flag 470), the clock watch in Update (the time-out at 0: line 60, entity 9 back to locator 11, the hero to locator 14, the replay prompt 59; the clock hidden after a game), the catch (lines 35 to 38, the time tier 471 / 472 / 473 by 120 and 60 s, the clock hidden, the placement, and the result ladder 39 to 42 that sees the tier just set), the quit prompt 211 (213 and the reset, or 212) from teleport 0 and the edge triggers; Update keeps object 0 on during a game; Exit stops, zeroes and hides the clock. The story ladders of routines 2 to 7 (flag 6 by Q, then 5, 4, 3, 2 and the local flags, with the 'then silent' lists and the flag sets 371, 373, 374, 376, 377, 379). Teleport 0: the quit prompt during a game, else with flag 374 the way to eW 70 (pack 947; the first time camera 4, locator 2, hero animation 162, sound 9, objects 10 and 11), else line 207. 1 test (the spec's line vector for entities 2, 1 and 3; the offer, a catch at 130 s setting 471 and 472 with line 40, the next offer at 180 s; the Exit's timer reset). Trace: slot 1 loads Claire (940); entering an area loads Old Man Boggs (937). Not built: the three-target game (entity 42's lines are not given), the clock's red blink (the clock is not drawn), the hiding places' navmeshes, the AI behaviours 0 and 5 (the entity is held and released instead), the ambush waves (threads 13 and 14: their character ranges are not mapped), threads 18 to 21.
- **Module 1394, the countdown course and hero-swap levers (SPEC-0041 1394):** Init sets objects 3 to 6, 10, 12, 14, 16 and 1 off, 7 and 8 at their end, registers the kill zones (0 to 10), S11 to S14 and the levers (15 to 18), and with flag 0 clear turns object 9 off, stops effect 3 and sets swap mode 3; the checkpoint starts at locator 0 with camera 2. The intro (S13, once) plays cameras 9, 10 and 12 with lines 2 to 5 and objects 7, 8 and 2, swaps to hero 0 and starts the 50 s countdown. Update sinks object 2 by (50 - t) x 0.46 (a new vertical object offset, SetAnimObjPositionOffset) while the countdown runs; at 0 the run-out scene (object 2 off, object 0 shown and played on camera 11) and the respawn. The levers (Square context 4, once per load) stop the clock, play hero animation 140, the lever and its camera, swap the hero (15: hero 1, checkpoint (1, 3); 16: hero 0, (15, 4); 18: hero 1, (16, 5); 17: hero 0, (8, 4) and the final word) and re-arm the clock. The kill zones respawn at the checkpoint (with the final word, at locator 8). S14 stops the clock for good; S11 sets 104 and 353 and the room word (movie 65 is not played); S12 with the room word loads eW 158. 1 test (the offset at 25 s, lever 16 once, the kill-zone respawn, S11 and S12). Not built: the dead-hero revive, the hero-death callback, the object sounds, movie 65.
- **Module 1392, gates and button levers (SPEC-0041 1392):** Init registers triggers 0 to 23 and 25 to 36, sets up the objects and, by flag 696, the three gate levers at their end (gates 11, 13, 15 at their start) or at their start with effects 13 to 18 stopped. Trigger 0 sets 696 (its only writer). The gates A to C (anim objects 10, 12, 14; cameras 5, 7, 9; return presets 11 to 13): triggers 1 to 5, 7 to 12 and 14 to 22 open them (the first of each run only while its lever is unpulled), triggers 6, 13 and 23 close them and show the gate object again when the lever was pulled. Levers 25 to 27 (Square context 4, once per load) move the hero to locator 16 to 18, play hero animation 140, the lever, camera 4, 6 or 8, sound 7 and two effects, and show the gate object. The nine helper levers 28 to 36 (helper D) play hero animation 141, the lever and its object on their camera. Update runs the fall-in once while 696 is clear (the hero to locator 0, 3 s, the camera back). 1 test (the fall-in once, trigger 0, helper lever 31 once, gate A open and close). Assumed: an open gate is not reopened and a closed gate not reclosed (the spec gives the open and closed state but not whether the helpers test it; Medium; R-0057). Not built: SetAI_Behavior 8 for the helper levers' AIs 11 to 15, the object sounds, the per-variant waits of helper D.
- **Module 1371, the wave trial (SPEC-0041 1371 branch B; SPEC-0059 section 8):** Init (a normal visit) stops effect 0, registers Z0 to Z2 on triggers 0 to 4 and sets object 1 at its start. The Z2 gates open the three waves in order (trigger 2: none won and object 1 still, locator 23, gate object 1, region 1378; trigger 4: 342 only, locator 25, object 3, region 1379; trigger 3: 342 and 343, objects 3 and 2 still, locator 24, object 2, region 1380): the walk, the fades, objects 10, 9 and 4, camera 8, sound 10 and the gate object, the slot emptied and loaded, and the wave word set. The wave controller (Update) scores a wave once every character of its slot is dead: flag 342, 343 or 344, object 10, sound 9, object 4 off, and with all three the camera 9 scene. Z1 (all three won, 100 and 345 clear) sets 345 and 100, turns object 8 off, starts effect 0 and opens Z0, which posts the won result (type 1 value 1, type 2 value 20 for eW 157) and loads eW 157 (pack 1350). TP0 (context 13): with 100 value 60 and the load of eW 157, else line 2. The activate-world callback moves the hero to locator 16 on value 60 for eW 163. Two new host signals: each slot's living count once its load has arrived (GetAssetGroupCharacterRange with IsAI_Dead) and the animating objects (IsObjAnimating). 1 test (the gate order, the living-count gate, object 1 animating, Z1 and Z0). Not built: branch A (the arena rounds from pack 1350, which is not built: the hero-death switch, threads T and V, the win and loss scenes), the flag-345 and flag-100 object gates of Init (their objects and poses are given only as 'mixed'; R-0058).
- **Module 1382, the six element pedestals (SPEC-0041 1382):** Init registers each pedestal's strike (CB1) and stand (CB2) triggers, sets up its objects and puts it out (the lit count 0), sets up objects 15 to 20 and the floor-switch objects, registers the floor switches (3 to 8), CB4 (2) and CB6 (9 to 14), and stops effects 2, 3 and 1. CB2 arms a pedestal while the hero's own path fires its cylinder. CB1 on a strike while armed: the weapon Mote (equipment word 2) of the pedestal's element (the record table: 168/169, 160/161, 166/167, 170/171, 162/163, 164/165) lights it after 0.5 s (sound 7, the five effects and two lit objects, the count up on a first lighting), anything else drops the lesser Mote at the drop locator once per load (AddItemToWorld, the locator form: a new script action, a placed item that never expires). The floor switches swap their object pair, raise the next marker on the first step on each (objects 15 + n and 21 + n, camera 4), swap back once the hero leaves and, all six stepped on, arm CB4, which sinks the markers and loads set 7 (region 1390) into the fight slot. CB6 loads set index - 8 (regions 1384 to 1389) when it is not the last loaded. Update: six lit, once per load, the completion (the fade, all six put out and locked, the listed entities off, the fight slot emptied, flags 98 and 337, object 28 off, effect 1, the exit routine on trigger 28, which fades and loads eW 178, pack 1411). 2 tests (the spec's vectors 1 to 6, the switch order and the set loads). A strike is the first tick of the hero's attack window (the frame bit 0x1) while the strike box fires: whether the host's trigger paths fire these boxes at all, and how the callback's attack word is written, is R-0059 (unverified in play). Deviation: a fade in follows the completion where movie 70 would play. Not built: CharacterActivate of the lit pedestals' fight-slot characters, the look-at-player camera 5 of CB4.
- **Module 1363, the Mortog shrine (SPEC-0041 1363):** Init sets objects 3 and 4 by flag 693 and objects 0 and 2 by flag 692 (the puzzle done), registers triggers 0 to 8, and with flag 106 clear loads set 0 (region 1364; thread M's first step). Trigger A, the pressure plate: object 2 eased down to step 40 one step a tick (the spec's curve, depth -0.25 over 40 steps; camera 4 the first time), object 0 shown and played, and after the hero leaves object 2 back to the puzzle level with object 1 and sound 8. Trigger B (context 6) plays objects 3 and 4 and sets 693; C (context 13) posts value 10 for eW 178 and 160 and loads eW 178; D, the shrine door, posts 20 for eW 174 and 160 and loads eW 174 with the puzzle done (or 692 or 355), else line 0; E (context 3) shows line 1 or 2; F with 106 empties the slot and loads set 3, 2 or 1 (regions 1367, 1366, 1365) on every entry. Teleports 0 to 2 (context 13): with 106 values 40, 50, 30 for eW 160 and the entry into eW 184, 185, 177, else line 0. W14 moves the hero to locator 1, 3, 5, 2 or 4 by the value for eW 160. The Mortog thread runs from Update on NumMortogsOnTarget: the shakes, the hold times (25 and 30, up 5 on odd rises), object 2 ten steps down on a rise and up on a fall (each move ten ticks, during which the count is not read), and at 4 the done scene and flag 692. 1 test (the spec's Mortog vector, the third rise, the finish and the shrine door, thread G's set 1, the return to locator 2). Not built: the Mortogs themselves (InitMortogBehavior and ChangeMortogTimes; the host's NumMortogsOnTarget stays 0, so in play the puzzle completes only with flag 692 already set), the proximity lines of entities 37 to 42 (their fallbacks are not fully given), the Square prompt of trigger D (none is given).
- **Modules 748, 749, 752 and 776, small interiors (SPEC-0043 748, 749, 752; SPEC-0037 776):** 749: Init posts value 40 for eW 20, the normal camera; on-enter (76: line 0 in the frame, else 2); proximity on entity 0 (76: line 4 in the frame, else 6 in the frame and 9); the entity-control record of entity 0 (idle 0, 2 to 5 s, dwell 5 to 7 s, 40, 96, 41 at 1.3 s). 752: proximity on entity 0 (266: line 0 in the frame; 265: 3; 76: 265 set and line 6; else 7) and entity 0's record with points 1 to 4 (dwell). 748: the normal camera and entity 0's record (40, 110, 96, 41); its two dialogue routines are not wired because the spec does not say which is the on-enter and which the proximity routine (R-0060). 776: teleport 0 (kind 13) posts 10 for eW 31 and leaves to eW 44; the arrival with 10 moves the hero to locator 1; its proximity routine is not wired (R-0061). 1 test. Not built: the item-ask routine of 749 (item-ask callbacks never fire, SPEC-0030 row 257), the blackboard messages of 748 and 752 (values not given).
- **Module 1257 (SPEC-0039 1257):** Init registers handler 1 (trigger 17) and handler 7 (trigger 0) and loads set 0 (region 1258). Handler 1: with 459 set, 77 clear, item 29 not held and level state 0 clear, level state 0 and line 0. Handler 7 (entering, 676 clear): the ambush scene (the fade, letterbox, the hero to locator 4 and the walk to 5, entity 0's idle 112, cameras 4 to 8, lines 17 to 19, the entity animations, entities 1 to 3 made aggressive, the letterbox off) and flag 676. Teleports 0, 2 and 1 (context 13) post 10, 20 or 30 for eW 147 and load eW 138, 139 or 140; the arrival moves the hero to locator 0, 2 or 1 by that value, else returns the camera. 1 test. Not built: handler 2 (triggers 1 to 16; its GetCollisInfo tests are not specified), handler 7's CharacterActivate and CharacterDeactivate (their character is module data). Medium in the spec: handlers 2 and 6 and the teleports.
- **Modules 757, 1250 and 1083 (SPEC-0039 757; SPEC-0042 1250, 1083):** 757: Init sets objects 2 to 5 at their end and registers handlers 1 to 3; handler 2 (trigger 1, 664 clear, once per load) closes the arena (objects 0 and 1, 4, 5, 2 and 3 played back, cameras 5 and 6) and makes entities 14 to 16 aggressive, and once all three are dead (from Update) opens it again (camera 7) and sets 664; handler 1 plays camera 4 at 0.85, fades and sets 665; handler 3 makes entity index - 2 aggressive. The spec gives handler 2's two branches flat (Medium); the win is taken as a wait for the three deaths. 1250: Init sets 267, objects 0 and 7 or 8 by flags 77 and 275, S1 on 17 to 19, E0 on 0, effects 0 to 9 stopped; E0: with 77 clear the pickup (item 29: the camera, shake and effect sequence, the item removed, line 6, 77) or the talk ladder (459: 4; 469: 3; 468: 469 and 2; else 468 and 1), then with 273 set and 275 clear the flag-275 scene (object 7 played back, camera 6, a wait while object 7 animates: a new WaitObj action); S1 posts 10, 20 or 30 for eW 143 and loads it on contact (hero animation 174); the arrival with 40 for eW 141 moves the hero to locator 26; the item-79 watch (530 set, 531 clear, four held: 531). Object 7's active test in E0 is taken as true (it is on whenever 77 is clear). 1083: with 620 set objects 5 to 19 at their end and object 1 at its start, else T0 (620, object 1, objects 5 to 19 played, object 0 off); thread A loads set 2, 3, 4 or 1 by flags 52, 20, 19 (region 1084 + set) into slot 0. Not built in 1083: the object setups of the 620-clear branch and the three shared ones (ids open), the 52 branch's second load. 1 test.
- **SetWorldCollisMask and module 1066 (SPEC-0043 1066; SPEC-0018 mask gate; SPEC-0030 row 212):** a world now keeps every contact-firing trigger volume and push box with its gate mask, and SetWorldCollisMask (a new script action) selects those whose mask is 0 or meets the word (0 at load, as before). The ground probe's mask gate is still not built. 1066: Init registers T2 (0 to 2) and T1 (3), sets the masks once per load from flags 17 and 414 (5, 1, 6 or 2; 16 set when 17 is clear) for collision and display, and runs the slot-set chooser (stages 20 to 16 by flags; sets into the four slots, region 1067 + set, with the 414, 145, 148 and 144 conditions). T1 (entering, 145 clear) shows line 4 + r or 6 + r (r = Random(0, 1), a host draw made only in 1066) and walks the hero to locator 75; T2 (context 3) shows line 10 + index with the button. Proximity: C1 (entities 17, 18: the 409, 410, 414 ladder with items 13 and 21), C2 (16: by 143 and 127, else 20, 21, 22 then 21 and 22 repeated), C3 (19: 144 set and a counter 0 to 3, then 1 to 3), each case cycling its lines and restarting when the case changes. Update turns object 0 off with flag 2. 1 test (the four masks, stage 16's loads, the C1 ladder, C2 and C3 orders, T2). Assumed: C3's counter values are its line ids 0 to 3 (the spec gives the counter but not the lines; lines 0 to 3 are otherwise unused; R-0062).
- **SetWorldLoadOverRide and modules 1427 and 1391 (SPEC-0006 Pending slot writer; SPEC-0030 row 217; SPEC-0037 1427, 1391):** a new script action stores the current world's pending slot when the argument is 0 to 192; the next pending step uses that world id's pack in place of the cell's exit value (the pair rule and the buffer rule as for a cell) and sets it back to -1. 1427: triggers 0 to 7 run E0 to E4 with no state or object test, and the shared helper sets slot 187 to 191 at most once per tick (Update clears the once-word): climbing the stairwell requests packs 1428 to 1432. 1391: S2 (entering) the camera shake (5.5, 0.3, 25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.5708, 0.2), object 0 played after 1.5 s and the hero held until it ends; S1 sets slot 165; S0 does nothing. 1 test (SPEC-0030's once-per-tick vector, slot 191, 165). Not built: 1391's look-at-player camera 4 (the normal camera is returned after the poll instead of before it), the re-assertion of a trigger-set slot while game-state word +0x44 is set (Medium in SPEC-0006), the 1184 and 822 sites (those modules are not built). Not verified in play: packs 1427 and 1391 are not reached by the traces.
- **Modules 908, 906 and 837 (SPEC-0037):** 908: Init's four object setups (its world-swap callback runs only from StartTransitionScene, which has no live call, so RegisterScriptWorldSwap needs no host). 906: trigger 2 (context 6) loads eW 65 with the button; L0 and L1 play camera 0 or 1 and return the camera; the pickup of item 24 sets 406; every activation cuts to black, moves the hero to locator 0, returns the camera and fades in over 1 s; thread T loads set 0 (region 907). 837: Init by flag 636: clear, object 0 and masks 1; set, masks 2 (collision and display). 1 test. Deviation: L0 and L1 run on entry only (the spec gives them no record test, so the original runs them on every call). 837's breakable watch and break scene were added later (the other modules' breakable calls).
- **Module 1350, the arena hub, and 1371's arena rounds (SPEC-0042 1350; SPEC-0041 1371 branch A):** 1350: Init registers T0 and S1 with flag 100, S2 and S3 always, and loads set 1 or 0 (region 1352 or 1351). The arena master (entity 24) offers the round by the result word and flags 595 to 600 (prompts 14 to 21, talk lines 13 and 4); no gives line 28 to 33, yes line 22 to 27, posts 20 for eW 157 and loads the arena (eW 163, pack 1371). P25 cycles its branch's lines (61 to 72) with a branch marker; P26 to P31 (shape X, bases 73, 90, 107, 124, 141, 158) pick by N (flags 596 to 600 set) against the host's Random(1, 6) draw with one shared counter. T0 shows entity 24's line 4 to 11; S2 (on entry) posts 10 and loads eW 161; S3 (context 3) shows lines 1 to 3; X0 and X1 (context 13) need flag 100 (values 30 and 50, eW 176 and 182; else line 0); X2 posts 60 and loads eW 163. The arrival moves the hero by the value (10, 30, 40, 50, 60: locators 4, 1, 2, 3, 5), and on 20 to locator 6 with the round-result step (a win sets the next of 597 to 600, sets 596 and gives two of item 147, lines 34 to 38; a loss sets 595, lines 39 to 43). 1371 branch A (value 20 for eW 157 at load): objects 10, 9 and 4, Sevaric (region 1377) and the round's set (1372 + t by flags 599 to 596), the intro reduced to a fade in, line H1 and entity 30's walk to locator 15; watcher V wins once every character of the round slot is dead (1 s, the win scene H5, swap mode 2, the won result and the load of eW 157). 1 test (SPEC-0042's vectors for P25, shape X and the round result; the offer, the round loads, the win and the return). Not built: the loss path (it needs the hero-death callback), the intro and loss scenes' moves and cameras (Medium in the spec), raising the heroes' hit points to 50 percent on return. Deviation: S2 runs on entry only (no state test in the spec).
- **Modules 714 and 772 (SPEC-0042):** 714: Init registers P (trigger 0; context 3, line 0) and, with 220 set and 301 clear, F (trigger 2: 301 set, 30 ticks, line 1, and with flag 5 clear line 2); stops effects 9 to 15 with 301 and 284 clear; thread A loads set 0 or 1 (region 715 or 716) by flags 394 and 395. Update sets masks 4 (collision) and 15 (display) while 220 is set and 284 clear. 772: Init registers X (trigger 0; context 3, line 46) and sets object 0 looped (543) or object 1; Update sets object 0 up and loops it again each tick with 543; C (entity 2) runs the 590, 591, 544, 543, 542 chain (lines 9 to 17; the 11 to 13 and 15 to 16 cycles share a counter that restarts when the rule changes); D (entity 14) sets 592 and runs the walk-off (lines 18 and 19, set 1 into slot 0, entities 9 to 13 walk to points 2, 3, 8, 6 and 4, the fade, slot 1 emptied, the five released and made aggressive); the activate-world callback loads set 2 (region 775) into slot 1 with 591 set and 592 clear. 1 test (714's gates and F once; 772's line vector and D). Assumed: D's 'slot 1' is the second slot (the one the activate-world load fills); the spec says 'the stored slot' for both loads (Medium). Not built: 772's item-pad handler E (its two extra record fields are not defined).
- **Module 1398, the five-stage area (SPEC-0043 1398), stages 1 to 3:** Init registers routines 1 to 5 (triggers 34, 38, 33, 35, 37), sets 355, sets the four Whinny pens (inner 7 to 10, entrances 11 to 14, locators 61 to 64), and with 106 clear sets up objects 0, 1, 2 and 5, stops effect 15, takes the done words from flags 720 to 724 and starts thread 6 for the first unfinished stage (set 0, 3, 1 with the Miamice field 15 and cage 16, or 2; region 1399 + set); with 106 set every stage is done. Set 5 (region 1404) is loaded into slot 1. Stage 1: trigger 38 arms the trap (objects 10 to 11, 1 to 4, camera 4) and keeps the hero's hit points (GetHitPoints, a new host value); a drop on a later tick trips it and it can be armed again; trigger 33 ends the stage (720, set 3). Stage 2: once AI 13 to 15 are seen dead, on the next tick camera 5 and objects 5 and 6, 721, set 1 and the Miamice. Stage 3: four Miamice caged (NumMiamiceInCage, a new host value from the catch count): camera 6, object 8 off, object 7, 722. Trigger 35 arms stage 4 (camera 10, object 8 to its end, set 4); trigger 37 with 356 moves the hero to locator 34 with effect 16; door 0 posts 20 for eW 160 and loads it (pack 1363). Entities 0 to 4 and 16 to 21 are undamageable; 16 to 18 are held at full hit points while stage 4 runs. 1 test (the pen vector, the trap arm and trip, stages 1 to 3, a reload's stage). Not built: stage 4's passes (trigger 34 runs only for a firer other than the hero, which needs CollideEntityWithTriggers for entities 16 to 21 and 9 to 12), stage 5 (the four entities' navmesh test: the spec does not list them, and GetEntityCurrentNavmeshID is not built), movie 67, the per-flag object setups of Init, the five entities' hit-reaction test of the trap, the stage 4 camera shake values and avoidance points. Deviation: trigger 37 runs on entry only.
- **Module 1411 (SPEC-0044 1411):** Init sets 95 with 99, 105, 101 and 103 on the first visit and 96 and 335 once 100, 102, 104 and 106 are all set, registers the triggers, runs the mosaic-camera thread's flag sets (688 to 691; its group cameras are not played) and the slot thread (98: set 0, else with 96 set 1). The mosaic (trigger 0): each held piece of items 25 to 27 is removed and its object (2 to 4) shown with camera 5; at three the mosaic completes (334, a fade, camera 4, sound 6), below three line 63. Triggers 17 (336 and 97, camera 13, line 66), 16 (338), 1 (with 338 the load of eW 191, pack 1432, in place of movie 72 and its return world), 18 (cameras 6 to 8, lines 64 and 65, 703), the exit 3 (button: value 10 for eW 160 and its load) and the signs 4 to 15 (button: lines 51 to 62, with 354, 340, 351, 347). E1 to E6 on entities 0 to 10 step through their groups of three by 338 and 337. Update shows line 67 the first time 25 to 27 are held together and, each tick, starts the effects of script-group instances 4, 1, 2 and 3 for flags 104, 106, 102 and 100 (a new GroupEffects action over the group's effect list). The villager table gets eleven adds of which the nine-record limit keeps nine. 1 test (the mosaic, sign 11, E2's groups, Update's vector, the nine records). Not built: the mosaic's object animation (its object is not given), the six object setups, the Square prompts of triggers 3 and 4 to 15 (none is given; the button alone acts). Assumed: script-group instance k is the pack's k-th script group; E1 to E6 restart their group of three when the group changes.
- **Module 842 (SPEC-0045 842):** the story stage (the highest of flags 59 to 53) picks the slot thread's set (59: 6, 58: 7, 57: 4, 56: 3, 55: 7, 54: 1, else 0; region 843 + j) and object 0. Init registers triggers 0 to 8, sets 197, shows object 1 while 198 is clear and arms the AI-kill watch while 54 is clear. The four doors (context 13) show line 196, 195 or 194 at stages 57, 55 and 53 and otherwise enter eW 55, 54, 57 or 56 (doors 0, 1, 2, 3; packs 856, 855, 858, 857). Trigger 1 sets 53 with the opening camera (no stage) or shows line 201 (stage 54); trigger 0 shows line 197 until 54; trigger 3 (54 set, 199 clear) runs entity 19's conversation (lines 206 to 212, cameras 16 and 17) and sets 199; trigger 2 runs the two quest scenes (55: sets 7, 2 and 3, item 9 taken, lines 68 and 168, flags 56 and 201; 58: sets 7, 2 and 6, lines 69, 164 to 166 and 200, item 126, flag 59); trigger 4 (57, 286 clear) shows entity 34's line 90; the signs 5 to 8 (context 3) show lines 202 to 205. The AI-kill watch (stage 53 or none) waits for AIs 4, 6, 8, 0, 1, 2 and 9 to die, then plays the scene (lines 198, 199), sets 54 and 198 and loads set 1. Update turns object 1 off with 198. The nine proximity routines (36 entities) follow the spec's tables, including template V, the 510 'first q/r' rotations, the 524 to 527 item 77 chain, the 516 to 519 item 75 chain with its two silent talks, and the dead line 113. 1 test (the stage loads, the doors, trigger 1, the AI-kill watch, template V, the 510 rotation, the silent talks). Not built: the AI movement types and villager socialising calls, the scenes' entity animations, entities 35 to 40's walk-off points. Deviation: the quest scenes fade in after each move (the spec lists fade outs with no fade in; Medium order).
- **Module 1335 (SPEC-0043 1335):** Init sets 235, registers the triggers, sets object 1 or 0 by 241 and runs the slot chooser (94: none; 93: set 2; 92: set 1 or 0 by 241; else set 7 into the second slot; region 1336 + set). The shared trigger (0 to 3, 92 clear) loads set 3 + index when it differs from the last; trigger 5 (context 32) with 235 set and 92 clear empties both slots, loads set 0 and sets 236 and 92; trigger 6 (236, 237 clear) shows lines 30 to 32 with camera 5; trigger 7 (240, 241 clear) swaps objects 1 and 0 and loads set 1; triggers 8 and 9 set 403 and 402. The doors (kind 13): 0 shows line 35 (236 set, 93 clear) or posts 20 for eW 151 and loads pack 1300; 1 (241 set, 242 clear) posts 80 for eW 150 and 155 and loads eW 150 (pack 1299) in place of movie 26 and its return world, else line 34; 2 (238 set, 239 clear) posts 70 for eW 153 and 155 and loads pack 1324, else line 33. - **The Gnarfas boss (SPEC-0020; behaviour class 14):** `neopets_sim::gnarfas` holds the controller with every tuning word read from the boot data at the spec's addresses: the spawn draws, the timers, the phase hub (event 0 when the hero is below half health or after three strikes; phase 2 once level states 0 and 1 are set, restoring the hero's health; the hold while the hero is busy; the one-shot rage below 25 percent; death below 1 percent, sending event 2 when its animation ends), taking damage only through the flinch (animation 9, the damage applied at its end; none during its attacks or the missile block), the missile reaction (block, dodge or jump by the phase's chances, then a 1 to 3 s timer), the melee dodge (three variants), the attack order (chain whip and rapid strike on their cooldowns within 9, the chain power's zero chances, the head butt within 15 by its phase chance), approach (run beyond 5, walk within) and the orbit (a coin side, the move point 3 units across), the chain whip and rapid strike (raw 15, once per attack, the block still taking full damage, knockback 1.0 unblocked), the head butt (event bit 0x1, ahead and within 7: knockback 2.0, a strike), the jump-to-tree sequence (steps 0, 1, 3, 2 and 5 with the 1.5 launch and the 0.05 gravity, the perch waits, the 50 percent jump at the hero) and the body slam (30 within 5, unblockable, the hero-down skip, the laugh roll). 5 tests. The host gives class-14 characters the controller and a walker: the hero's swing and shots reach the boss as a pending hit, the missile paths are tested against its body cylinder, the bone-chain test of the frame's event bits uses the posed bones (radius 0.5, length 1.25 against the hero's 1.0 by 3.0 cylinder) with chain spark 710 at the run's tip, the tree points are locators 101 to 104, travel is the clip's root motion with the shared steering rule (modes 5 and 6 face across the move) or the jump's velocity, and the hits use the hero's take-hit with the 8.0 push. On the disc (`--pack 985 --play-trace 6000 --trace-to fight`): Gnarfas approaches, orbits, chain-whips the hero for 15, rapid-strikes, exhausts and runs the tree jump; with the hero below half health it sends event 0, and pack 985's thread plays its movie-12 step and line 14 ('Gnarfas looks like he's fire aligned, so I should use a water mote on my sword'); without a water Mote the hero's hits do no damage. Not verified: the defeat (event 2) in play, phase 2's heal in play. Not built: the missile reaction's bone cylinders (the body cylinder, radius from the monster row and 5.0 tall, stands in), the block-missile pose (event 0x14; the idle clip stands in), the orbit's blocked-side flip and walk-mesh test, the one-time phase-2 effect, the sounds of block 393.
- **The Werelupe King (SPEC-0019; behaviour class 32):** `neopets_sim::werelupe` holds the controller with the tuning words read from the boot data at the spec's addresses: the pre-check (the hit flinch, one of 0x0B to 0x0D; the catch; triumph while the hero is down; the phases from the health fraction, the phase-2 entry posting event 4 and the phase-3 entry event 6, with the phase-2 regression quirk; death, setting flag 273), the cooldown counters (running in every state; the flame counter stopping at 601), the reaction choices on the hero's rising attack edge (block, held while the hero attacks, with the overhead counter; the jump with the home-distance ramp 5 + (d - 15) / 13 x 75, uncapped; the roll with its always-passing counter-attack that keeps the once flag), the timed attacks in order (boomerang, flame wave, swing alternating 0x17 and 0x16, lunge, combo) with their windows and phase chances, the movement (walk beyond 25, the hop timer and the not-ready hop back, the orbit within 10 that never ends by itself, the side vector (-u.z, 0, u.x)), the shared once-per-attack hit (the AI attack-active frame bits, the reach test, the guard at dot -0.5 or less taking 0 with the blocked flag), the combo's three hits with the flag cleared at its transitions, the jump flight (v = (dx/60, 1.5, dz/60), gravity 0.05, landing within 3 of home), the boomerang (the release, waypoints A and B at 0.5 D u ± 0.4 D s, the hero point at ground + 2, the hand, the weights 1.0, 0.25 and 0.1 by distance, the 0.7 step, the ground floor, 5 raw once per throw, the 600-tick timeout) and the five flame rings (effect 415's size rule r = 0.5 + 24.5 t / L and its two-rate height, the strict 0.6 band and dy < h, 15 raw once per ring per volley; four waves, five in phase 3, waits of 0 to 15 frames). 4 tests (the spec's phase, ring growth and reach, jump flight, side vector, jump ramp, steering weights, once-per-attack, guard, combo, roll counter, walk threshold and flame gate vectors). The host gives class-32 characters the controller and a walker: the hero's hits apply at once and flinch the King next tick, his own controller handles his death, the reach test is the 2.5 by 7.0 weapon cylinder from 1.5 behind a chest-high anchor along the facing against the hero as one 1.0 by 3.5 cylinder (the anchor and the hero's body records are not given; Medium), the hand point is 1.1 ahead at 3.0 up, ring effect 415 starts at each ring centre, and the travel and steering are as for Gnarfas. Deviation: the phase-2 regression is applied only between 37 and 75 percent (the spec's statement that a phase-3 boss returns to phase 3 only below 37 percent; a literal reading of its else-if chain would make a phase-3 boss below 37 percent alternate phases every tick). Not verified in play: in pack 1246 the King loads inactive and the trace route does not reach the arena entrance, so the fight has not run on the disc (R-0064). Not built: the boomerang's model and spin, the hero body shape records (the boomerang and reach tests use a hero cylinder), the jump's no-gravity flag and the landing fall, the orbit's blocked jump, the stinger for flag 273, the clip sounds of block 367.

Stage 1 does nothing and is not emitted. - **The Mote-eater drain (SPEC-0034 3.13; SPEC-0011):** `sisters::Drain` with the words read from the boot data at 0x2DCAC8 (7) and the per-Mote effect table at 0x2DCAE8 (14): the start's 8 picks from Motes 160 to 171 (a repeat steps to (pick mod 12) + 1; a walk past 11 gives 12, no slot, so 7 or 8 slots), each slot's effect from the table, the countdown trunc(30 x 60) + 1 = 1801 and the period countdown / slots; each call counts down, stops everything at 0, and reveals the next slot when the countdown is a multiple of the period (offset (U(-a, a), 4.0, U(-a, a)) with a = 0, using 20 charge of its Mote under the switch word). The pool sends a Mote-eater event after an unblocked kind-14 hit; the host starts the drain with body effect 464 4.5 above the hero, steps it once per sister loop still stepped (so k sisters run it k times faster), keeps the effects on the hero, spends the charge (a spent or missing Mote is cleared from the equipment) and ends the effects at the stop. 1 test (the spec's 8-slot and 7-slot reveal and stop vectors, the pick's 7 or 8 slots). Not verified in play (the sisters' special missiles need them below a third of their health). Not built: the slot effects' 0.5 scale.
- **The Ixi Chief's controller (SPEC-0014; behaviour class 11; sim only):** `neopets_sim::ixi` holds the controller with the tuning words read from the boot block at 0x2DAE90 (47 words, the base damage scale last): the fight mode from game states 358 and 60 (scale 0.25 in mode 0, else 1.0), the cooldowns, mode 0's stages (the dormant hold until level flag 0 with the three cooldowns drawn, the transition below half health with level flag 1 until level flag 2, death with level flag 3), mode 1's lava (the first below 0.66 setting both latches, so the second never fires; level flag 2 on its action frames) and death with level flag 0, the hold while level flag 3 is set, the missile and melee reactions (in the free states and the attacks, not the charge's start and run-past steps), the incoming hits (none in the hold, lava or death; the facing guard of the dodge, block, swipe and get-hit states) into the get-hit state with its 25-in-101 forward swipe, the chooser (counter within 6, the charge with no distance test, the forward swipe, the wide swipe on a strafing hero, the buck kick behind, the whirl in mode 1 only), the movement band with its 8.0 and 13.0 hysteresis, the orbit (3.0 to the side, the flip every 30 ticks, the navigation check), the charge's seven sub-steps (the candidates along the hero's forward and both sides, the slam roll below 6.0, the run past to 7.5 with its push) and the hits (weapon 1 for the swipes, counter and slam, weapons 4 and 5 for the buck kick, the whirl's range, the forward swipe's facing block, trunc(tuned x scale), unreduced). 3 tests (the spec's mode, damage, push, orbit, candidate, band, stage, lava, block, whirl, hit, charge and wide-swipe vectors). Not wired into the host: its movement states and the charge's walk need the locomotion clips (R-0067), without which the charge would stall at its walk step.
- **The Black Knight's controller (SPEC-0012; world pack 989; sim only):** `neopets_sim::knight` holds the per-fight record (state, phase, speed multiplier, counter flag, the six weak points from the boot block at 0x2DA3E8, the charge references +20/-20/+60/-60) with the tuning words read from that block (56 words): the glow pulse from the previous tick's lit parts, the flag decision (laugh, angry and enraged starts with their phases, loosen neck, en garde with its speed multiplier), the defeat test (all six at zero or below: state 19 and level state 0 once), the parry (Tor, the attack predicate and its latch) and deflect (Roberta, the first active shot's overlap, the 240-to-360-tick stance), the selectors in order (the opening kick at any distance, the guard kick, the slow and angry swings by phase, the phase-2 charge with its direction test, high swing, floor strike and neck spin, the balcony spin off the knight's mesh), the idle movement (turns past the threshold, approach, the orbit with its 30-tick side flip), the swings with the facing block and their phase moves, the four enraged attacks' sub-steps with their 600-tick stun windows and part hits (floored at 0, reaction animations 44 to 47, effect 305), the flying sword's sub-states (attach, launch, orbit with its bounce and turn counts, homing, return) and the health display's quirk (the right knee counted twice, the right under-arm never). 5 tests (the display, defeat, charge-test, knock-back and glow vectors; the selection order and flags; the charge's exits and the stun window; parry, deflect and the turns; the sword's homing and return). The rolls are 0 to 100 inclusive. The flying sword's orbit offset is built from the knight's facing (the spec's pose-derived basis is Low) and its launch turn is approximated as a fixed drop, so its world path is unverified. Not wired into the host: the approach and orbit need the locomotion clips (R-0067), and the weak-point cylinders need the posed part frames.
- **Trace diagnostic NEOPETS_TRACE_ROBERTA=1:** a trace starts as Roberta (the exchange with the parked hero), so `--give 111` gives her a wand and the fight trace's attack fires her shots. In pack 1122 (`--flag 31 --flag 320 --start -10,-113,-10 --give 111`) she launches shots on ticks 648 and 773; they fly along her facing and pass about 2.5 units from the sisters, so the shield break is still not reached by the trace bot (its aim, not the game rules).

The arrival stops effects 15 to 21, returns the camera, places the hero at locator 0 and sets stage bit 0. Thread W1 (from Update, one helper a tick): the arrival step (the fade, the loads, the equip, bit 1), the level-state request (the spec's vector: level state 1, 2 or 3 gives bit 2, 3 or 4, the next pass bit 5), section 1 (barrier groups 50 to 53, 42 to 49 and 22 and 23 raised), section 2 (the scene with camera 7 and 8, entities 1 to 3 to locators 41 to 43, the barriers changed) and section 3 (camera 10, effects 9 to 14, entities to locators 53 to 55, only group 29 and 30 left raised). A raised barrier's trigger hurts the hero by 3 at most once every 1.5 s. Trigger 1 (any fire, once) runs the Act 2 start: flags 219 to 221, act start 2 and the load of eW 151 (pack 1300) in place of movies 16, 89 and 18 and their return world. 1 test. Not built: the floor tiles, checkpoints, breakables and two-hit object (they are fired by shots and the act-1 primitive path and use the DFS_Act1 calls), the barrier sounds, the bits 8, 9, 17 and 18 scenes, the Faerie Sisters and the Darkest Faerie's loop A, the boss-fight tweaks. Assumed: barrier group g's damage trigger is 30 + g (the spec lists the six triggers and the seven groups but not their pairing).
