- **The Recruitment Centre's entity mapping, the shield and the hilt (SPEC-0039 1148 Init and handlers 4 and 5; SPEC-0042 1150 P4; SPEC-0058 step 7):** fixed a blocker in pack 1148. Its proximity routines are numbered in handler order (0 to 3 for handlers 2 to 5), but entities were mapped to routines by their own number. So entity 3, Tark Gutterunner, ran the plain three-line rotation that belongs to entity 2 (the unloaded slot character), and the shop step that sells item 42 (the sword hilt) could never run. That left flags 140 and 644, and through them the sword and Torak's sponsorship, out of reach. Entity 3 now runs routine 2 and entity 2 routine 3; the test checks the mapping. Traces: in Armourer Inga's shop (1131) the Wooden Shield (item 106) is placed for sale at 400 NP; the buy is refused with 0 NP and works with 500. A scan of every pack from 700 to 1440 finds item 106 placed only there and in pack 1259, which SPEC-0006 lists as a cut map. Kendrick accepts the shield (138). Torak in the tavern (1150) sets 139. Tark now offers the hilt (lines 41 and 43), and paying 100 NP gives it and sets 140 and 644 (line 44). docs/PLAYTEST-ACT1.md has the shield step and the developer menu's Neopoints entry.
- **Thrown model and the Mote glow (SPEC-0046 Second walk step 1 and Held items step 4; SPEC-0019 Boomerang flight; SPEC-0023 section 25):** while the Werelupe King's boomerang flies, his slot 0 model is drawn at the boomerang's position instead of in his hand, spinning 0.6 radians per tick (interpretation: about the vertical, since the spec's eased spin axis is open; R-0086). Not checked in play: the trace route does not reach the arena (R-0064). The Black Knight's flying sword is not drawn because his controller is not in play. Tor's weapon and shield Mote glow is not built: the specs disagree on which ids resource 9 registers (SPEC-0021 says 191 to 282, SPEC-0023 says the glow models 299 to 311 are resource 9's models in 283 to 322) and the glow's blend is not given (R-0086).
- **The Ixi Chief in play (SPEC-0014; class 11; pack 800, region 806):** the controller now runs in the host. A class-11 row gets a walker and the controller with the fight mode from game states 358 and 60, and the tuning block is read at boot. Each tick the host gives it the hero's attack, guard and state, the missile contact (a hero shot's 120-tick path against the boss's body cylinder, widened by the shot's radius), the melee hit and the summed shot damage it took (a hit on it goes to its controller, which applies the HP loss through its get-hit state), the monster weapon tests of attach points 0 to 4 on the clip's event bits, the run-past block, the hero's knock state, the navigation check against its mesh and the level flags. It plays the controller's clip (the locomotion slot entries of R-0067 while it travels), turns toward the hero, steers by its locomotion mode and moves only by the clip's root travel, with the height-check lock word on its walker (heights more than 1.0 apart), hits the hero (blocked flag passed), pushes it (the knock), sets level flags, takes its HP loss and plays the hit flash 222. In a trace with level flag 0 set, the Chieftan wakes from the dormant hold, rushes, charges (walk entry 30, then 22), forward-swipes and buck-kicks the hero (12, 6 and 25 damage), and the hero's sword hits take 10 each through the get-hit state. Not checked: the half-health transition (level flag 1), the lava of mode 1 and the death (level flag 3, which module 800's stage D waits for), because the trace's fighter loses. Interpretations: the movement modes other than the orbit steer at weight 0.2, the boss's body for the missile test is its row radius and height, and the hit flash sits 2.0 above its feet.
- **The Ixi Chief's navmesh and walk (SPEC-0029 Behavior 2 Navmesh pick, Movement and Steering by mode; SPEC-0056 Navigation step; SPEC-0014 Charge sub-steps):** the boss now picks its navmesh each tick its stored mesh no longer holds it (the first ground mesh holding its position, as class 17 does). Before, it had none, so every charge point counted as valid and it charged off its raised arena after the hero. Its walks in modes 0 to 4 (the charge walks, rush forward, strafe close and strafe back) now run the walker's SPEC-0056 navigation step toward the move point instead of a straight turn at an invented weight of 0.2, and modes 3 and 4 steer toward minus the direction (`Walker::reverse`; they used to steer forward). The orbit modes 5 and 6 are unchanged. The trace print of characters with no enemy controller now shows the HP, the Ixi state, the position and the inactive and held marks. Disc traces in pack 800 (NEOPETS_TRACE_CHEATS=god, NEOPETS_TRACE_TARGET="Ixi Chieftan", --flag 60 for fight mode 1; without 60 the boss holds in mode 0, as specified): from the default start the hero is stopped by a wall at x 428.9 and the boss stays on its platform (y 306.8); from --start 520,2412 the fight runs, the first lava event comes at HP 190 (tick 29331), and on tick 35261 the boss stops in charge sub-step 1, 3.3 from its point: each tick it is pushed off a boundary edge of mesh 6 (blocked), the settled branch's fresh block reports arrival and turns it toward the hero, and sub-step 1 ends only within 1.0 (asked as R-0088). Unverified: the half-health stage change, the second lava event and the death in play.
- **Draw and sheathe sounds (SPEC-0040 Behavior 29; SPEC-0031 Weapon slot uses):** a change of the weapon-drawn flag (an attack start, the guard, a hit, Square with nothing to interact with, a talk or the swap) now plays the weapon slot's cue 0 (unsheathe) when it is set and cue 1 (sheathe) when it is cleared, while the hero has a weapon. Not checked by ear.
- **Lighting (SPEC-0046 Light slots and the lit draw; Scene default lights):** `light.rs` holds the scene default rig (ambient 50 and three directional lights at strength 0.25) and the lit vertex formula (L = A + Σ clamp(n·T, 0, 1) C; RGB = min(255, L × byte / 16)). Lit parts (programs 1, 2 and 6) are lit at build time in the space they are built in; skinned characters are relit every frame from their skinned normals turned into world space. Not implemented: the draw colour scale, program 6's specular alpha. Character lights are the next bullet.
- **Character lights (SPEC-0046 Character lights, Character light slots, Light slots and the lit draw; SPEC-0028 sections 2 and 8):** `neopets_data::light::light_sets` reads every type-30 chunk (records in sequence after the table, the table words unused, the type from the array). `--lights` census: 140 packs, all ending exactly at the payload end, 2,449 records (151 gated) and entries A 535, B 2,507, C 223, the spec's counts; 685 has 29 records and records 0 and 1 hold the spec's values (gate 1 and 0, type 2 flag 1 strength 0.15, direction (0.1005, -0.9917, 0.0801), colour (203, 255, 143)). `neopets_sim::charlight` has the four-slot state: reset, the four scene default lights as own entries, pass 1 (type 2, then the first type 1 entry of each ungated record), pass 2 (type 3), the dynamic list with the dynamic rule; the zone and radius gates, the type-3 falloff and cross-fade lock, the displacement candidate, the radius-0 point light treated as absent, the colour fill with the 5 % ease of kind-0 slots (replace when dynamic or kind 1), and the block (type-3 direction normalize(model - light)); 7 tests with the spec's vectors. In play the walk and fill run once per tick after the effect pools for the hero and every region character over the current world's light set, and the hero and characters are relit from their own block (the default rig before a first walk). The walk runs only for a character on a ground entry whose zone passes the zone query with its world id, and each entry passes the query with key -1 (zone grids, next bullet). Trace: before the zone gates, 685's hero ambient eased to 0.2 x (128, 110, 83) = (25.6, 22.0, 16.6) and slot 1 to 0.15 x (203, 255, 143) (record 1, zone 6); with them, the spawn's zone 1 does not see zone 6 (block 0 row 1 is 1110110) and the hero keeps the default rig, as the data say; 909 has no light set. Dynamic point lights: `Effects::lights` lists, after the effect tick, each live particle of a light emitter whose node has a material as its spawner's type-3 entry (particle position, channel 1 x 255, strength channel 1 alpha, radius channel 2 x and its square), k times for k live particles with the last particle's values (the original's repeat, kept), at most 200, and the walk takes them with the dynamic rule; 1 test. `--effects-check` counts 37 light-emitter records and 167 definitions, the spec's numbers, but all 167 light-emitter nodes have material -1, so under SPEC-0034 4.5's material condition none publishes a light on disc (effects 1300, 2202 and 4012 run 60 ticks and list none; R-0051). Deviations and gaps: the petpet has no walk of its own; characters of the other resident world use the current world's set.
- **Zone grids and the zone query (SPEC-0006 Binary grids, Zones, Zone manager, Query, Edge cases; SPEC-0060 Behavior 1; SPEC-0046 Character lights):** `neopets_data::zone` reads the type-35 blocks (key, W, H, cells, and the byte past the cells); `--lights` also counts them: 120 chunks, 324 blocks, every block 0 square and keyed -1, as the spec says. The ground probe now returns the chosen entry's zone (the low byte of entry +0x0C). `neopets_sim::zone` has the manager (R stored only when z is below the active block's height and below 255) and the query (no grid passes; block 0 for k = K, the active block for k = -1, the keyed block otherwise or pass; i < 0 or i > N passes, i = N reads the byte past the cells); 2 tests. Each world keeps its grid with block 0 keyed by its world id (area table). Each tick before the foliage step K is the current world's id and R the zone of the hero's ground entry (camera mode 0's follow target), with block 0 active. Users: the character light walk and entry gates, and the foliage gate (column = the patch zone, key -1 in the current world, the world id in the other one): a patch that fails is not drawn and runs no bush logic. The character cell flag (SPEC-0006 Cell flag) is recomputed each tick for every region character (1 without a ground entry, else the query with its entry's zone and its world id); a character whose flag is 0 is treated as inactive that tick (not updated, drawn or hit), and script deactivation is kept apart so that both apply; the live petpet is not gated. Trace: in 909 R = 1, 92 of 102 patches are on and 4 of 11 characters are off; in 1023 R = 47 and 54 of 59; in 757 14 of 20 characters are off at the spawn and the fight route still agroes and hits a Dumb Skeleton on tick 535; the Meerca chase (following Meerouladen) still reaches every waypoint and sets 136 on tick 9253. Not used yet: the streaming release of cell-off characters, the cell gate of unbound records, the placed-object draw gate (its zone key byte 0x1B8 has no known writer, R-0050), the camera world's grid when the camera stands in the other world, and the second world's active block while its objects draw.
- **Model viewer:** the selected resource's node trees are drawn double-sided and unlit with their materials; the camera frames the model, and the diagnostic grid is hidden while a model is shown. `V` switches between the model and the texture quad. World packs frame on where most geometry is (median vertex and the radius covering 60 % of vertices), and WASD/Q/E (Shift faster) move the orbit target as a fly camera.
- **Inspector:** `--texture <n>` writes the resource's textures as PNGs under private/textures/ (a small built-in PNG writer, `png.rs`), `--materials` resolves and decodes every top-level material on the disc, and `--meshes` decodes every node tree and checks each leaf against its header and bounds.
- **Viewer scaffold (groundwork for M3/M4/M6, no spec):** the app now renders through a 3D orbit camera (drag any mouse button to orbit, scroll to zoom, pitch clamped short of the poles) over a diagnostic grid with X/Y/Z axes, labeled on screen as not game content. Tab hides the HUD. This is viewer tooling, not the original game's camera. Input mapping and the browser picker moved into `neopets-app/src/controls.rs`, orbit math into `orbit.rs`, both unit-tested; Check.cmd now runs `cargo test -p neopets-app` instead of only `check`.
- **GS pixel formats (groundwork for M3, public hardware documentation only):** `neopets_data::gs::decode` turns linear PSMCT32, PSMCT16, PSMT8, and PSMT4 pixels (with 32- or 16-bit palettes, CSM1 or linear) into straight RGBA8, with GS alpha scaling (0x80 = opaque) and TEXA handling for 16-bit alpha. Size-checked (1..=1024 per side), no panics on short input. Not covered: PSMCT24, GS memory swizzled layouts, mipmaps. Whether the game uses any of these, and how, still needs R-0003.
- **Viewer test pattern:** a 64×64 4-bit indexed pattern generated in code is decoded through `gs` and shown on a quad, labeled synthetic. `--screenshot <png>` captures the window after 4 s of real time (render pipelines compile asynchronously, so earlier frames show no meshes), and `--hide-hud` starts with the HUD hidden. The HUD uses ASCII separators because Bevy's built-in font lacks `•` and `—`.
- **Clean-room split (M1):** research tooling moved to `../neopets-research/`; `specs/` channel, request list, template, and spec audit added. See docs/CLEAN_ROOM.md.

## Verified

- Before the split: 4 core tests passed (synthetic ISO reads/ranges, malformed images, missing program, mod precedence, path traversal). Real ISO recognized as SCUS-97367 v1.00, 100 files indexed, no extraction. Full Bevy build and `--smoke-test` exited 0 (startup only, not visual review).
- After the split: see the check log below.

## Not implemented / not verified

- No mod UI yet; audio is partial (streams, bank music, hero, creature, item, menu and placed cues, stingers) (specs for all of these are approved). Textures and models decode, but only the parts of SPEC-0003 and SPEC-0004 listed above.
- SPEC-0003's description of the upload segment's buffer-width bits does not match the disc (R-0008); the decoder uses width / 64, which the decoded images confirm.
- Nested chunk walking (type-8 and type-32 children) is not surveyed yet; only top-level chains are.
- The optional GAMEFILE.DAT and SYSTEM.CNF fingerprints in SPEC-0001 are not checked (the boot-program digest is the required one).
- Live mouse orbit, Tab, the keyboard picker, and gamepad input have not been tried in a running window.

## Progress tracking

Progress.cmd and docs/PROGRESS.md: code coverage (research-side specs/PROGRESS.md, first delivered with this batch), asset coverage (`neopets-data --coverage` now writes private/asset-coverage.txt: 1358 non-empty resources in 8 families, 0 decoded), and pipeline/milestones (M0–M2 done).

60 specs approved (SPEC-0001 to SPEC-0060), copied in by the user on 2026-10-25 and passing the clean-room audit. SPEC-0001 and SPEC-0002 are Implemented; the rest are Approved. R-0001 to R-0007 are answered.

- 2026-10-25 (M7 groundwork): Check.cmd passed: 52 data unit tests (new: synthetic ground probe accept, ceiling and fallback rules, spawn height rule), 8 data integration, 19 sim (new: speed value, basis example, the moving-turn angle sequence from 90°, tier speeds, turn in place at 135°, ground follow, run restart wait), 11 app, both audits. `--ground` on the real ISO: pack 909's spawn record (-1852.8, 50.0, 1137.5) facing (0.6, 0, -0.8) gets ground height 40.5 with x-major cells; level-mesh vertices hit within 0.5 units 164,871 of 909,642 times x-major against 29,648 z-major. `--play --autowalk`: Tor spawns on the ground at 909's entry record and walks along his facing at 0.158 units per tick (walk 0.2251 × tier factor 0.7), following the ground; screenshots private/screenshots/m7-*. He walks through fences (no contacts yet).
- 2026-10-25 (dome and story layers): Check.cmd passed: 54 data unit tests (new: dome slot rule, first-row slot lookup, layer-flag gating in the tree walk), 8 data integration, 19 sim, 11 app, both audits. `--areas` on the real ISO: 193 slots over 191 packs; SPEC-0006's sample rows (slots 0, 5, 68, 107, 192) match; the dome rule picks exactly SPEC-0046's pack lists for domes 2 and 3. Screenshots: pack 1023 without the story-gated vortex and with dome 1's sky; play mode unchanged at ground level.
- 2026-10-25 (lighting): Check.cmd passed: 56 data unit tests (new: SPEC-0004's lit vertex vectors (148, 148, 156, 128) up and ambient 50 down; SPEC-0046 slot colours; saturation), 8 data integration, 19 sim, 11 app, both audits. Screenshots m5-r504-lit and m7-lit show Tor shaded by the default rig while walking.
- 2026-10-25 (box walls): Check.cmd passed: 56 data, 8 data integration, 23 sim (new: SPEC-0028's synthetic box outside, low-A push to -r, shallow top, negative extent with radius 1.25 and 1.0), 11 app, both audits. `--ground` census: 6,524 rectangles parse (SPEC-0028's count), unit axes within 6e-8, 4,883 pass the box-push gate at mask 0. A 15 s autowalk in 909 crossed no box on its path; it climbed a slope to y 62 because terrain walls (frame records) are not implemented. `--screenshot-at <s>` sets the capture time.
- 2026-10-25 (frame-record walls): Check.cmd passed: 56 data, 8 data integration, 25 sim (new: SPEC-0018's Petpet wall vector, two-sided, and the span, height and face-tilt gates), 11 app, both audits. `--ground` census around 909's spawn: 14,446 frame-record references (with repeats), all with unit face and run normals and near-vertical faces, types 0, 1, 2, 3 and 6. The same 15 s autowalk in 909 now stops against a fence at y 41.4 instead of climbing to y 62. Boxes now push with the hero radius 1.175.
- 2026-10-25 (gravity and jumps): Check.cmd passed: 56 data, 8 data integration, 31 sim (new: fall-law drops, resting, landing friction and snap, ground projection, the standing-jump curve, the state-10 drift distances, the walk-off timings), 11 app, both audits. `--play-trace 200 --autojump` in 909: the jump on tick 121 peaks at gap 3.203 and lands 47 ticks later (phase 5 on the 46th tick, moving landing on the 47th), as in SPEC-0033's flight curve; a 1,800-tick walk without jumps never leaves the ground states. The hero jumping at the fence it walks into does not clear it.
- 2026-10-25 (follow camera): Check.cmd passed (sim 39 with the camera's 8 new tests). In 909 the camera settles at distance 9.74 and elevation 0.200 behind the standing hero, as SPEC-0027's settle vector ends, and trails the hero during an autowalk.
- 2026-10-25 (camera profiles and line of sight): Check.cmd passed: 57 data (new: segment versus frame record), 8 data integration, 42 sim (new: profile choice, line-of-sight recentre on the 61st tick, recentre shrink on a blocked line), 11 app, both audits. `--pack <n>` starts play mode in another world pack. In 909, 735 and 758 the camera stays on profile 0 along the traced walks; 735's and 732's record-0 points are not over sampled ground (735 falls onto ground nearby; 732 stands at the probe's 0.0), so those packs need their door arrival points.
- 2026-10-25 (overworld streaming): Check.cmd passed: 59 data (new: swap set size, neighbour lookup offset), 8 data integration, 43 sim (new: buffer stream rule), 11 app, both audits. `--areas` travel census as above; `--play-trace 4000 --trace-to swap` swaps 909 to 691 between ticks 961 and 1,020 of a 44-step route.
- 2026-10-25 (background stream loads): `--play-trace 9000 --trace-to swap` walks from 909 onto 691 (swap), then reroutes to a 691 cell naming a non-resident neighbour: exterior 934 is loaded on the loader thread with its scene and becomes the other world, replacing 909. A cell of 909 naming interior 951 is read once and not streamed, as the buffer rule says. Check.cmd passed (59 data, 8 integration, 43 sim, 11 app, both audits).
- 2026-10-25 (doors): door traces: 772 door 0 to transition 799 (its door 0), back to 772 and into 799 again; interior 857 leaves to door 3 of 842, as SPEC-0006's example says; 893 enters interior 859 at entry record 0 and 859's door returns to 893's door 0; 990's door replaces the exterior with paired 986, arriving at door 1. Check.cmd passed (59 data, 8 integration, 43 sim, 11 app, both audits); play mode starts in 893 in the window.
- 2026-10-25 (farm doors): `--play-trace 3000 --pack 909 --trace-to door`: 909's door 0 enters 951 (slot 72); 951's door 0 leaves to 909, whose activate-world routine moves the hero to locator 2 (beside door 0), and T is reloaded with 691 (slot 5). The door touch reach is now the body radius plus 1.5: in 951 the wall stops the hero 2.66 from the exit door's plane (R-0012 updated). The default-door traces of 772, 857 and 893 still pass. Check.cmd passed: 59 data, 8 integration, 46 sim (new: farm doors and returns, round-up reset, stage-6 placement), 11 app, both audits.
- 2026-10-25 (level lines): with `--flag 119`, 951's exit door shows line 126 (SPEC-0041) from the pack's line table instead of leaving. Check.cmd passed: 61 data (new: control-code stripping, line records), 8 integration, 46 sim, 11 app, both audits.
- 2026-10-25 (region characters): `--play-trace 4000` loads regions 910, 914, 920 and 926 on the region loader (the inspector found kind-2 table ranges were payload-relative; fixed). In the window with `--look` toward Lucy's placement, the farm family stands in front of the house and animates. Check.cmd passed: 62 data (new: region record walk), 8 integration, 47 sim (new: farm slots by stage), 11 app, both audits.
- 2026-10-25 (talk): `--play-trace 3000 --trace-to " -1860.4,1131.5"` walks to Lucy and presses Square on the Talk prompt: lines 159, 160, 161 in turn (routine C's new-game branch), each held until the next press. Check.cmd passed: 62 data, 8 integration, 50 sim (new: routine A and B vectors, rotation reset), 11 app, both audits.
